UNIVERSITY: UNIVERSITY OF STUDIES GABRIELE D'ANNUNZIO – CHIETI/PESCARA
COURSE: [0658] FOREIGN LANGUAGES AND LITERATURES
SECTION: INFORMATION ON STUDY PLANS
URL: https://unich.coursecatalogue.cineca.it/corsi/2024/2

INFORMATION ON STUDY PLANS General information relating to the current academic year. Duration: 3 years. Admission year: 2022. Status: Open. Credits: 180 CFU. Language: Italian. Course type: Degree course. Regulatory reform: 270/04. Degree class: L-11 - Class of degrees in Modern Languages and Cultures. Inter-university course: No. Open access. Other locations: PESCARA (Sustainable users: 139). Department: DEPARTMENT OF LANGUAGES, LITERATURES AND MODERN CULTURES. Website: https://www.lingue.unich.it/didattica. Admission requirements: Required knowledge for access. Those who possess a five-year secondary school diploma or another equivalent qualification obtained abroad, as recognized by current regulations, are admitted to the L11 degree program. Access to the course of study is regulated by an assessment of knowledge and skills through a specific verification test. The methods for verifying these knowledge and skills will be determined in the didactic regulations of the course of study. In the event of a negative outcome of the verification, additional training obligations will be assigned to be fulfilled during the first year of the course through specific remedial activities established by the didactic regulations. Admission procedures: All students who possess a five-year secondary school diploma or another recognized equivalent qualification are admitted to the course. Upon enrollment, the student must indicate the chosen languages of study as LANGUAGE A and LANGUAGE B (one of which must be European) from those available in the Course Offer. The three-year language (both Language A and B) can only be changed upon enrollment in the second year. The Ministry of Education, University and Research (Article 6 of DM 270/2004) requires all universities to verify the initial preparation of new students, in order to allow students to assess their personal preparation with respect to the basic knowledge required for university studies, and to identify, also with the help of tutor teachers, individual paths aimed at filling any initial gaps, thus promoting the student's successful integration into the chosen course of study. The ministerial provision also clarifies that any training debts must be recovered during the first year of the course. For the L11 degree program, the Verification Test of the knowledge required for access is mandatory for students enrolled in the first year. All students transferring from other universities and students enrolling in a second degree are exempt from the Test. The Verification Test is held according to a calendar indicated on the website of the Department of Languages, Literatures and Modern Cultures. Two sessions are scheduled: in September and in February. The Commission responsible for the Test is appointed by the Director of the Department. This Commission is made up of a minimum of three and a maximum of five permanent teachers. The Verification Test, lasting 60 minutes, consists of 30 multiple-choice questions aimed at ascertaining an adequate level of text comprehension, knowledge of the morphological-syntactic structures and vocabulary of the Italian language. Each correct answer is awarded a score of 1 (one), while each unanswered or incorrect answer is awarded a score of 0 (zero). The test is considered passed if the student obtains a score of no less than 20/30. For the student who takes the Test and passes it, the Additional Training Obligations will be considered fulfilled. A negative outcome of the test, however, entails the assignment of an Additional Training Obligation (ATO) to be fulfilled during the first year, by attending a mandatory 24-hour course in Italian language offered by the Department. At the end of the remedial procedures, the student is required to repeat the Test, in order to ascertain the required levels of competence. The debt must be fulfilled within the first year of the course. If the debt is not fulfilled, the Verification Test must be repeated in the following academic year. As per the university's didactic regulations, students who have not fulfilled the ATOs by October 31 of the year following enrollment cannot enroll in the courses of the second year, unless they have alternatively obtained at least eighteen CFU in the basic and characterizing courses of the first year. In addition to the Verification Test of the knowledge required for access, a Placement Test is also administered to ascertain the level of linguistic competence in a language chosen from French, English, Spanish and German. This second Test is used to optimize the didactic organization of the courses and does not therefore entail Additional Training Obligations.

UNIVERSITY: UNIVERSITY OF STUDIES GABRIELE D'ANNUNZIO – CHIETI/PESCARA
COURSE: [0659] LINGUISTIC MEDIATION AND INTERCULTURAL COMMUNICATION
SECTION: INFORMATION ON STUDY PLANS
URL: https://unich.coursecatalogue.cineca.it/corsi/2024/563

INFORMATION ON STUDY PLANS General information relating to the current academic year. Duration: 3 years. Admission year: 2022. Status: Open. Credits: 180 CFU. Language: Italian. Course type: Degree course. Regulatory reform: 270/04. Degree class: L-12 - Degree class in Linguistic Mediation. Inter-university course: No. Open access: Yes. Other locations: PESCARA (Sustainable users: 169). Department: DEPARTMENT OF LANGUAGES, LITERATURES AND MODERN CULTURES. Website: https://www.lingue.unich.it/didattica. Admission requirements: Required knowledge for access. Those who possess a high school diploma of five years' duration or another qualification obtained abroad, recognized as suitable according to current regulations, are admitted to the Course of Study. Access to the course of study is regulated by a specific verification test, the methods of which will be determined in the Didactic Regulations of the Course of Study. In the event of a negative outcome of the verification, additional training obligations will be assigned to be fulfilled during the first year of the course through specific remedial activities established by the Didactic Regulations.
Admission procedures: All students who possess a high school diploma of five years' duration or another equivalent qualification are admitted to the Course. At the time of registration, the student must indicate the chosen languages of study as LANGUAGE A and LANGUAGE B (one of which must be European), from those present in the Course Offer. The three-year language (both Language A and B) can only be changed at the time of registration for the second year.
The Ministry of Education, University and Research (Article 6 of DM 270/2004) requires all Universities to verify the initial preparation of new students, in order to allow freshmen to assess their preparation with respect to the basic knowledge required for university studies and to identify, also with the help of tutor teachers, individual paths aimed at filling any initial gaps, promoting the student's good integration into the chosen course of study. The ministerial provision also provides that any training debts are recovered during the first year of the course.
For the L-12 Degree Course, the Verification Test of the knowledge required for access is mandatory for students enrolled in the first year. All students transferring from other universities and students enrolling in a second degree are exempt from the Test.
The Verification Test will be held according to a calendar that will be published on the website of the Department of Languages, Literatures and Modern Cultures. The scheduled sessions are two: September and February.
The Commission responsible for the Test is appointed by the Director of the Department, after consulting the President of the Degree Course. This Commission is made up of a minimum of three up to a maximum of five permanent teachers.
The Verification Test, lasting 60 minutes, includes thirty multiple-choice questions aimed at ascertaining an adequate level of text comprehension, knowledge of the morphological-syntactic structures and the vocabulary of the Italian language. A score of 1 (ONE) will be assigned to each correct answer, while a score of 0 (ZERO) will be assigned for each unanswered or incorrect answer. The test will be considered passed if the student obtains a score of no less than 20/30.
For the student who takes the Test and passes it, the Additional Training Obligations will be considered fulfilled. A negative outcome of the test results in the assignment of an Additional Training Obligation (ATO) to be fulfilled during the first year, by attending a mandatory 24-hour course in Italian language offered by the Department.
At the end of the remedial procedures, the student will be required to repeat the Test, in order to ascertain the required levels of competence. The debt must be repaid within the first year of the course. If the debt is not repaid, the Verification Test must be repeated in the following academic year.
As per the University's didactic regulations, students who have not fulfilled the ATOs by October 31 of the year following enrollment will not be able to enroll in the courses of the second year, unless they have acquired, as an alternative, a number equal to at least eighteen CFU in the basic and characterizing courses of the first year.
In addition to the verification test of the knowledge required for access, a Placement Test will also be carried out to ascertain the level of linguistic competence in a language chosen from French, English, Spanish and German. This second Test will be used to optimize the didactic organization of the courses and will therefore not involve Additional Training Obligations.
The carrying out of both tests may, possibly, take place remotely.

UNIVERSITY: UNIVERSITÀ DEGLI STUDI GABRIELE D'ANNUNZIO – CHIETI/PESCARA
COURSE: [0684] SCIENZE E TECNICHE PSICOLOGICHE
SECTION: INFO PIANI DI STUDIO
URL: https://unich.coursecatalogue.cineca.it/corsi/2024/4

INFO PIANI DI STUDIO General information regarding the current academic year. Duration: 3 years. Program start year: 2023. Status: Open. Credits: 180 CFU. Language: Italian. Course type: Laurea (Bachelor's Degree). Regulatory framework: Reform 270/04. Degree class: L-24 - Class of degrees in Psychological sciences and techniques. Inter-university course: No. Limited access (611 places). Location: CHIETI (Sustainable capacity: 611). Department: DIPARTIMENTO DI PSICOLOGIA. Website: https://www.disputer.unich.it/cdl-scienze-e-tecniche-psicologiche. Admission requirements: Required knowledge for admission. The prerequisite for enrollment is a high school diploma required by current regulations or another degree obtained abroad, recognized as suitable by the competent bodies of the University. Enrollment is also allowed for those who hold diplomas issued by four-year institutions where the supplementary year is not active.
Admission to the Bachelor's Degree in Psychological Sciences and Techniques is regulated by a specific announcement that may include passing an entrance exam.
Possessing adequate initial preparation in the areas outlined below is considered a facilitating element for successfully completing the proposed training program. These areas are: a) Italian language; b) logical-mathematical area; c) area of humanities and social sciences; d) area of physical-chemical-biological sciences. These skills are assessed through a test with multiple-choice questions aimed at verifying the possession of adequate initial preparation in the areas mentioned above; this test may also be used for selection for the limited number of places.
In case of a score below a threshold, possibly set in the admission announcement, in the aforementioned test, enrollment is not precluded (until all available places are filled), although these students will be assigned Additional Training Obligations (OFA) to be completed according to the methods described in the Course's Educational Regulations.

UNIVERSITY: UNIVERSITÀ DEGLI STUDI GABRIELE D'ANNUNZIO – CHIETI/PESCARA
COURSE: [0685] PSICOLOGIA
TYPE: Laurea Magistrale
SECTION: INFO PIANI DI STUDIO
URL: https://unich.coursecatalogue.cineca.it/corsi/2024/9

INFO PIANI DI STUDIO General information regarding the current academic year. Duration: 2 years. Academic year: 2023. Status: Open. Credits: 120 CFU. Language: Italian. Course type: Laurea Magistrale. Regulations: Riforma 270/04. Degree class: LM-51 - Class of Master's Degrees in Psychology. Inter-university course: No. Limited access (120 places). Location: CHIETI (Sustainable capacity: 120). Department: DIPARTIMENTO DI PSICOLOGIA. Website: https://psicologia.unich.it/home-lmpsi-4609. Admission requirements: Required knowledge for admission. Admission to the course requires the possession of a Bachelor's degree or equivalent qualification (Italian or foreign) documenting a study curriculum consisting of at least 90 CFU in the field of psychological scientific-disciplinary sectors (M/PSI). These CFU must be distributed as follows: a) a minimum of 18 CFU in total in the sectors M-PSI/01, 02, 03; b) a minimum of 6 CFU in the SSD M-PSI/04; c) a minimum of 12 CFU in total in the SSDs M-PSI/05, 06; a minimum of 12 CFU in total in the SSDs M-PSI/07, 08. Further admission requirements are: (a) knowledge of the English language at a minimum level of B1, certified by passing an English language exam at a university level or by an external language certification; (b) obtaining 10 CFU of Practical-Evaluative Internship, certified by the curriculum of the Bachelor's degree; pending the application of Law 163/2021 and subsequent amendments, those who have obtained a degree in Scienze e Tecniche Psicologiche (L-24) under the previous regulations and have not completed the 10 CFU corresponding to the professional training activities, may request recognition of activities carried out and certified during the three-year course (DM n. 654/2022, art. 6 co. a and b), as well as request provisional registration and obtain the 10 CFU, in addition to the 120, during the Master's course (D.M n. 654/2022, art. 7). The adequacy of the individual preparation is verified through the weighted average of the grades obtained in the exams of the Bachelor's degree, referring to the aforementioned SSDs M-PSI, useful for the preparation of a merit ranking.

INFO ON STUDY PLANS General information regarding the current academic year.
Duration: 2 years
Academic year: 2023
Status: Open
Credits: 120 CFU
Language: Italian
Course type: Master's Degree Courses
Regulation: 270/04 Reform
Degree class: LM-51 - Class of Master's Degrees in Psychology
Inter-university course: No
Programmed access (216 places)
Location: CHIETI (Sustainable capacity: 216)
Department: DEPARTMENT OF PSYCHOLOGY
Admission requirements: Required knowledge for admission. To be admitted to the Master's Degree Course, the student must:
1) hold a Bachelor's Degree in one of the following classes: L-24 (ex DM 270/2004); L-34 (ex DM 509/1999);
2) or hold another degree obtained abroad, recognized as suitable according to current regulations;
3) have acquired at least 95 CFU in the following scientific-disciplinary fields: at least 18 CFU in the fields M-PSI/01, M-PSI/02, M-PSI/03; at least 6 CFU in the field M-PSI/04; at least 9 CFU in the fields M-PSI/05, M-PSI/06; at least 12 CFU in the fields M-PSI/07 and M-PSI/08;
4) have acquired knowledge and competence in the English language at least at level B1.
Pursuant to Article 2, paragraphs 6 and 7, of DM 654/2022, for the purposes of enrollment in the Master's Degree course, those who have obtained a degree in Psychological Sciences and Techniques - class L-24 under the previous regulations and who have not completed the professional training activities corresponding to 10 CFU of TPV may request recognition of activities carried out and certified during the three-year degree course according to the methods defined in the Didactic Regulations.
In the absence of, or partial recognition of, the CFU, three-year graduates must acquire the missing CFU for the internship in addition to the 120 CFU of the Master's degree.
Regardless of the curricular requirements, for all students, assessments of the adequacy of personal preparation and competence in the English language are required for admission, with methods defined in the Didactic Regulations.

UNIVERSITY: UNIVERSITÀ DEGLI STUDI GABRIELE D'ANNUNZIO – CHIETI/PESCARA
COURSE: [0692] SOCIAL WORK
SECTION: INFO ON STUDY PLANS
URL: https://unich.coursecatalogue.cineca.it/corsi/2024/585

INFO ON STUDY PLANS General information regarding the current academic year. Duration: 3 years. Program: 2017. Status: Open. Credits: 180 CFU. Language: Italian. Course Type: Degree Course. Regulatory Framework: 270/04 Reform. Degree Class: L-39 - Class of degrees in Social Work. Inter-University Course: No. Open Access: Yes. Location: CHIETI (Sustainable users: 93). Department: DEPARTMENT OF BUSINESS ECONOMICS. Website: https://class.unich.it/home-class-4001. Teaching Method: Conventional. Knowledge Verification Required for Access: Yes. Course Lecturers: View list. Admission Requirements: Knowledge required for access. With reference to the curricular requirements, all those who possess a secondary school diploma obtained in Italy or abroad and recognized as suitable, according to current regulations, may access the Social Work (L-39) degree program. It is also required to have a good knowledge of spoken and written Italian, good logical reasoning skills, and basic knowledge in social sciences and current affairs. The methods for verifying these knowledge and skills will be determined in the didactic regulations of the degree program. Any educational gaps found will result in the assignment of Additional Training Obligations (OFA) to be completed after enrollment, during the first year of the course, according to the methods indicated in the Didactic Regulations of the course of study. Admission Procedures: With reference to the curricular requirements, all those who possess a secondary school diploma obtained in Italy or abroad and recognized as suitable, according to current regulations, may access the Social Work (L-39) degree program. For the evaluation of adequate personal preparation, the Department Council appoints a Commission that verifies, through an interview, the possession of the required knowledge. If the Commission deems the candidate's level of knowledge to be sufficient, it expresses a judgment of suitability, which allows enrollment. Those who have obtained a score of at least the minimum threshold to ensure the possession of the required requirements and preparation are exempt from the interview. This minimum threshold is set at 70/100. If this threshold is not reached, the candidate will be required to take an evaluative interview with the Commission appointed to verify the possession of the necessary knowledge. Any educational gaps found in one or more of the following areas of study: a) Basic knowledge in social sciences and current affairs (SSD SPS/07), b) Approach to the scientific methodology of social sciences (SSD SPS/07), c) Communicative and logical argumentation skills (SSD SPS/08) will result in the assignment of Additional Training Obligations (OFA) to be completed after enrollment, during the first year of the course, through tests or verification interviews entrusted to the individual Lecturers of the aforementioned areas of study. The OFA are considered fulfilled if the students, who have not passed or have not taken the interview, have obtained at least 18 CFU related to basic and characterizing courses in the first year of the course and can therefore be enrolled in the second year.

Admission Requirements: Required knowledge for admission. With reference to the curricular requirements, all holders of a three-year degree in Social Work (L-39) or another degree obtained abroad, recognized as equivalent, are admitted to enrollment in the Master's degree program in Policies and Management for Welfare (LM-87).
Furthermore, holders of any three-year degree or equivalent foreign qualification, who have a total of 30 CFU in one or more of the following scientific-disciplinary sectors (SSD): SPS/07, SPS/08, SPS/09, SECS-P/07, SECS-S/01, SECS-S/05, SECS-S/06, M-PSI/04, M-PSI/05, M-PSI/06, M-PED/01, M-DEA/01, IUS/09, IUS/17, IUS/10, SPS/02, SPS/03, SPS/04, SPS/06, SECS-P/02, SECS-P/10, with at least one-third of the CFU in sociological sectors, as they are professionalizing for the type of studies, and the remaining CFU in other sectors, may also enroll in the Master's degree program in Policies and Management for Welfare (LM-87).
With reference to the verification of the adequacy of the personal preparation of candidates, admission to the Master's degree program in Policies and Management for Welfare (LM-87) is subject to evaluation through an interview by a Commission appointed by the Course Council.
If the Commission deems the level of knowledge and skills of the graduate to be sufficient, it expresses a judgment of suitability, which allows enrollment.
Graduates who have obtained a score of at least the minimum threshold to ensure the possession of the required requirements and preparation are exempt from the interview. This minimum threshold is set at 80/110.
If the curricular and knowledge level of the candidate, although deemed suitable by the Commission, is nevertheless deemed to require further in-depth study, the aforementioned Commission may allow enrollment by directing the candidate to deepen specific curricular subjects.
For anything not provided for in this article, reference is made to the current regulations and the University Didactic Regulations.
Admission Procedures: With reference to the curricular requirements, all holders of a three-year degree in Social Work (Italian or foreign, in any case recognized as equivalent/equivalent by the Course Council) are admitted to enrollment in the Master's degree program in Policies and Management for Welfare (LM-87).
Furthermore, holders of any degree or equivalent Italian or foreign qualification, who have a total of 30 CFU in one or more of the following scientific-disciplinary sectors (SSD): SPS/07, SPS/08, SPS/09, SECS-P/07, SECS-S/01, SECS-S/05, SECS-S/06, M-PSI/04, M-PSI/05, M-PSI/06, M-PED/01, M-DEA/01, IUS/09, IUS/17, IUS/10, SPS/02, SPS/03, SPS/04, SPS/06, SECS-P/02, SECS-P/10, with at least one-third of the CFU in sociological sectors, as they are professionalizing for the type of studies, and the remaining CFU in other sectors, may also enroll in the Master's degree program in Policies and Management for Welfare (LM-87).
With reference to the verification of the adequacy of the personal preparation of candidates, admission to the Master's degree program in Policies and Management for Welfare (LM-87) is subject to evaluation through an interview by a Commission appointed by the Department Council.
If the Commission deems the level of knowledge and skills of the graduate to be sufficient, it expresses a judgment of suitability, which allows enrollment.
Graduates who have obtained a score of at least the minimum threshold to ensure the possession of the required requirements and preparation are exempt from the interview. This minimum threshold is set at 80/110.
If the curricular and knowledge level of the candidate, although deemed suitable by the Commission, is nevertheless deemed to require further in-depth study, the aforementioned Commission may allow enrollment by directing the candidate to an appropriate personalized path, aimed at acquiring the further knowledge and skills deemed useful for the successful admission to the Master's degree program, through the deepening of subjects indicated by the Commission itself, with appropriate checks.

Access to the Master's degree in Planetary Sciences is open to all graduates, Italian and foreign, in the Geological Sciences class L-34 or in the Environmental and Natural Sciences and Technologies class L-32; to graduates, Italian and foreign, in other Technical-Scientific degree classes with at least 12 CFU in SSD GEO; or to graduates, Italian and foreign, with other equivalent foreign degrees recognized as suitable.

IN ENGLISH:
Access to the Laurea Magistrale (Master's degree) in Planetary Sciences is open to all graduates, Italian and foreign, in the Scienze Geologiche class L-34 or in the Scienze e Tecnologie per l'Ambiente e la Natura class L-32; to graduates, Italian and foreign, in other Techno-Scientific degree classes with at least 12 CFU in SSD GEO; or to graduates, Italian and foreign, with other equivalent foreign degrees recognized as suitable.
Furthermore, knowledge and competency in the English language at level B2 of the QCER is required.
For all candidates, an evaluation of their scientific geological competencies and English language competency will be conducted in accordance with the provisions indicated in the Didactic Regulation of the Course.

UNIVERSITY: UNIVERSITÀ DEGLI STUDI GABRIELE D'ANNUNZIO – CHIETI/PESCARA
COURSE: [3FF7] PHILOSOPHY AND EDUCATION SCIENCES
SECTION: INFO ON STUDY PLANS
URL: https://unich.coursecatalogue.cineca.it/corsi/2024/619

INFO ON STUDY PLANS General information regarding the current academic year. Duration: 3 years. Program: 2012. Status: Open. Credits: 180 CFU. Language: Italian. Course Type: Degree Course. Regulatory Framework: 270/04 Reform. Degree Class: L-19 - Class of Degrees in Education and Training Sciences. Interclass: L-5 - Class of Degrees in Philosophy. Inter-University Course: No. Open Access: Yes. Location: CHIETI (Sustainable capacity: 200). Department: DEPARTMENT OF PHILOSOPHICAL, PEDAGOGICAL, AND ECONOMIC-QUANTITATIVE SCIENCES. Website: http://www.disfipeq.unich.it/.
Admission Requirements: Required knowledge for admission. To access the degree course - Education Sciences program - students must possess adequate general knowledge and the basic knowledge required by the educational path of any upper secondary school or other degree obtained abroad, recognized as suitable. The degree course includes specific training activities for the acquisition of basic knowledge related to education and training sciences, aimed, in particular, at those students who have not studied them in upper secondary school.
The verification of the knowledge required for admission will take place through a multiple-choice test (and/or other type of test) for those enrolled in the Education Sciences program, which is not selective, on general culture content, on basic disciplinary content provided for in the educational paths of any upper secondary school, and on basic knowledge of the disciplines that characterize the degree course. Any negative results will not affect the possibility of enrolling in the course. To compensate for any learning gaps, specific additional activities are planned in the disciplines that characterize the degree course, to be followed in the first year of the course in support of the ordinary training activities.
To access the degree course - Philosophy program - students must possess adequate general knowledge and the basic knowledge required by the educational path of any upper secondary school and certified by the corresponding degree obtained in Italy, or by another degree obtained abroad and recognized as equivalent. At the beginning of the course of study, the possession and extent, within the scope of the general culture required for admission, of the most elementary philosophical notions and skills will be verified, in particular, through an interview and/or a written test. Any negative outcome of the verification will not affect enrollment in the course. If total absence or insufficiency of even minimal basic philosophical culture is ascertained, specific additional teaching activities will be offered on the disciplines that characterize the degree course, which the student must follow in support of the ordinary training activities.

INFO PIANI DI STUDIO General information regarding the current academic year.
Duration: 3 years
Program: 2012
Status: Open
Credits: 180 CFU
Language: Italian
Course Type: Laurea (Bachelor's Degree)
Regulation: Riforma 270/04
Degree Class: L-18 - Class of degrees in Economics and Business Management Sciences
Inter-university Course: No
Open Admission: Yes
Other Locations: PESCARA (Sustainable capacity: 109)
Department: DIPARTIMENTO DI ECONOMIA
Website: https://clea.unich.it
Teaching Method: Conventional
Verification of knowledge required for access: Yes
Course Lecturers: View list
Admission Requirements: Knowledge required for access. To be admitted to the Bachelor's Degree program, it is generally necessary to have a five-year secondary school diploma or another equivalent qualification obtained abroad, or a four-year secondary school diploma and the related supplementary year.
Specifically, the following are required for admission to the Bachelor's Degree program in Business Economics:
- knowledge of the Italian language and possession of related language skills as contained in the curricula of all types of secondary schools;
- a general culture that allows understanding and contextualization of the fundamental phenomena of the society in which economic entities operate;
- inclination towards economic and business disciplines;
- basic knowledge of English;
- basic logical-mathematical and computer skills.
The Bachelor's Degree program includes an admission test as provided for in Article 6 of Decree 270/2004.
The test for verifying the knowledge required for admission to the Bachelor's Degree program is considered passed if the student obtains a score equal to or greater than a minimum score set by the Board of the School of Economics, Business, Legal and Social Sciences. In the absence of an explicit decision, the minimum score set in the previous year is considered adopted.
A negative result in the knowledge verification test leads to the assignment of an Additional Training Obligation (OFA), which consists of attending a remedial course offered on the knowledge required for access, held in the first semester.
Verification of the completion of the OFA takes place with the passing of a minimum of 18 CFU, related to courses provided in the first year of the study plan, within the scope of basic, characterizing and related subjects, by the extraordinary examination session of the first academic year.
In the event of failure to complete the OFA within the established deadline, students must still complete the OFA and re-enroll in the first year of the course.
Transfers, changes of course, and admission to individual exams are subject to approval by the Course Council. The Council may grant full or partial recognition of the academic career pursued up to that point, with the validation of exams taken and credits acquired, and indicates the year of the course to which the student is enrolled and any OFA to be completed.

UNIVERSITY: UNIVERSITÀ DEGLI STUDI GABRIELE D'ANNUNZIO – CHIETI/PESCARA
COURSE: [613L] ECONOMIA E COMMERCIO
SECTION: INFO PIANI DI STUDIO
URL: https://unich.coursecatalogue.cineca.it/corsi/2024/566

INFO PIANI DI STUDIO General information regarding the current academic year. Duration: 3 years. Program start year: 2011. Status: Open. Credits: 180 CFU. Language: Italian. Course type: Laurea (Bachelor's Degree). Regulatory framework: Reform 270/04. Degree class: L-33 - Class of degrees in Economics. Inter-university course: No. Open access: Yes. Other locations: PESCARA (Sustainable capacity: 83). Department: DIPARTIMENTO DI ECONOMIA. Website: https://clec.unich.it/home-clec-66. Teaching method: Conventional. Verification of knowledge required for access: Yes. Course lecturers: View list. Admission requirements: Knowledge required for access. To be admitted to the Bachelor's Degree in Economics and Commerce, applicants must have obtained a high school diploma or another equivalent qualification obtained abroad and recognized as valid. To be admitted to the Bachelor's Degree in Economics and Commerce, applicants must also pass an initial knowledge verification test. The didactic regulations of the course of study govern the methods of verifying the required knowledge and the additional training obligations to be fulfilled in the event of a negative outcome of the verification. In particular:
1. The test for verifying the knowledge required for access to the degree course, pursuant to Article 6 of Decree 270/2004, is considered passed if the student obtains a score equal to or greater than the minimum set by the Board of the School of Economics, Business, Legal and Social Sciences. In the absence of an explicit decision, the minimum set in the previous year is considered adopted.
2. A negative outcome of the knowledge verification test results in the assignment of an additional training obligation (OFA), which consists of attending a remedial course offered on the knowledge required for access, held in the first semester.
3. Verification of the fulfillment of the OFA is carried out by passing a minimum of 18 CFU, related to courses provided in the first year of the study plan, within the scope of basic, characterizing and related subjects, by the extraordinary examination session of the first academic year.
4. In the event of failure to fulfill the OFA within the deadline referred to in paragraph 3 above, students must still fulfill the OFA and re-enroll in the first year of the course.

-----

To be admitted to the degree program, applicants must hold a five-year secondary school diploma or an equivalent foreign qualification recognized as suitable, or a four-year secondary school diploma and the related supplementary year. Furthermore, a good level of general culture, logical reasoning skills, competence in understanding texts in the Italian language, and basic knowledge of mathematics are also required (and verified). The methods for verifying the required knowledge for admission are regulated by the Didactic Regulations of the study program. The outcome of the verification does not preclude enrollment and may involve the assignment of additional learning requirements to be met according to the methods established by the Didactic Regulations of the course.

UNIVERSITY: UNIVERSITÀ DEGLI STUDI GABRIELE D'ANNUNZIO – CHIETI/PESCARA

TYPE: Master's Degree
SECTION: INFO ON STUDY PLANS
URL: https://unich.coursecatalogue.cineca.it/corsi/2024/3105

INFO ON STUDY PLANS General information for the current academic year. Duration: 2 years. Admission year: 2022. Status: Open. Credits: 120 CFU. Language: Italian. Course type: Master's Degree. Regulations: Reform 270/04. Degree class: LM-56 - Class of Master's Degrees in Economics. Inter-university course: No. Open access: Yes. Other locations: PESCARA (Sustainable capacity: 25). Department: DEPARTMENT OF SOCIO-ECONOMIC, MANAGEMENT AND STATISTICAL STUDIES. Website: https://esc.unich.it/home-esc. Admission requirements: Required knowledge for admission. To be admitted to the Master's Degree course in Economics and Behavioral Sciences, students must possess a level of English language proficiency no lower than B2 of the Common European Framework of Reference for Languages (CEFR). The methods for verifying English language skills will be defined in the Course Regulations. Students must also hold a three-year Bachelor's or University Diploma:
a) in one of the following classes according to D.M.270/04:
- L-16 BUSINESS ADMINISTRATION AND ORGANIZATION
- L-18 ECONOMICS AND BUSINESS MANAGEMENT
- L-33 ECONOMICS
b) or in one of the classes according to D.M.270/04 different from those previously listed, provided that a total of at least 30 CFU have been obtained in one or more of the Scientific-Disciplinary Sectors SECS-P/01, SECS-P/02, SECS-P/06, SECS-P/07, SECS-P/08, SECS-P/09, SECS-P/10, SECS-S/01, SECS-S/02, SECS-S/03, SECS-S/05 belonging to Area 13 (Economic and Statistical Sciences) and/or in one or more of the Scientific-Disciplinary Sectors IUS01, IUS04, IUS05, IUS07, IUS09, IUS10 belonging to Area 12 (Legal Sciences), with a minimum of 15 CFU in Area 13;
c) or in one of the degree classes according to D.M.509/99 identified as equivalent to those previously indicated by the Interministerial Decree of July 9, 2009 - Equivalencies between degree classes DM 509/1999 and degree classes DM 270/2004, for the purposes of participating in public competitions - Published in the Official Gazette of June 7, 2009, No. 233;
d) or, according to the previous four-year system, in one of the following degrees: Degree in Economics and Commerce, Business Economics, Political Economy and equivalent;
e) or in possession of another academic qualification obtained abroad, recognized as suitable.
For students who meet the curricular requirements, the Course Council will verify their personal preparation. The specific method of this verification will be defined in the Course Regulations.

SECTION: INFO PIANI DI STUDIO
URL: https://unich.coursecatalogue.cineca.it/corsi/2024/571

INFO PIANI DI STUDIO General information regarding the current academic year.
Duration: 2 years
Regulation: 2012
Status: Open
Credits: 120 CFU
Language: Italian
Course Type: Laurea Magistrale
Regulation: Reform 270/04
Degree Class: LM-77 - Class of Master's Degrees in Business and Economics
Inter-university Course: No
Open Access: Yes
Other Locations: PESCARA (Sustainable capacity: 100)
Department: DIPARTIMENTO DI ECONOMIA
Website: https://cleam.unich.it/home-cleam-75
Admission Requirements: Required knowledge for admission. To be admitted to the Master's Degree Course in Business Economics, applicants must hold a Bachelor's degree.
Admission to the course is also subject to obtaining a predetermined number of CFU in specific subject areas (SSD). In particular, all those who have obtained the following educational credits (basic, characterizing, qualifying, or chosen by the student) in their Bachelor's degree, which are deemed appropriate for the intended profiles, may be admitted to the Master's Degree Course in Business Economics:
a) SECS-P/07 (minimum 18 CFU)
b) SECS-P/08; SECS-P/09; SECS-P/10; SECS-P/11; SECS-P/13 (minimum 18 CFU)
c) MAT/02; MAT/05; MAT/06; MAT/09 SECS-S/01; SECS-S/03; SECS S/06 (minimum 18 CFU)
d) IUS/01; IUS/04; IUS 07; IUS/09; IUS/10, IUS/14; IUS/12 (minimum 18 CFU)
e) SECS-P/01; SECS-P/02; SECS-P/03; SECS-P/06; SECS-P/12 (minimum 18 CFU)
A tolerance of up to 10% (i.e., 9 CFU) is allowed. This tolerance margin may apply to one or more of the aforementioned groups of SSDs.
Furthermore, knowledge of at least one language of the European Union, in addition to Italian, is also required, both in written and oral form.
The verification of these requirements takes place through a specific and formalized verification process. In particular, each application for enrollment is examined by a special commission of the Course, which evaluates the individual academic careers of the students (if necessary, also through a verification of the teaching programs) in order to ascertain the existence of the admission requirements.

Admission Requirements: Required knowledge for admission: To be admitted to the Master's Degree Course in Legal Sciences for Internationalization and Business Innovation (Giurinn), applicants must hold a Bachelor's degree or a three-year university diploma in the degree classes listed below, or hold another degree obtained abroad, recognized as equivalent.
All those who have obtained a three-year Bachelor's degree or a single-cycle Master's degree in the degree classes L-14 Legal Services and LMG/01 - Law, according to DM 270/04 and DM 509/99, may be admitted to the course. Graduates of the following degree classes may also be admitted: L-18 - Economics and Business Management; L-33 - Economics; L-16 - Public Administration; L-36 - Political and International Relations; L-39 - Social Services; L-40 Sociology, according to DM 270/04 and DM 509/99.
For graduates in degree classes other than those listed above, admission to the course is subject to obtaining at least 48 university credits in specific SSDs (basic, characterizing, related and complementary, or chosen by the student) in the following 3 groupings:
1) At least 24 CFU in the sectors: IUS/01, IUS/02, IUS/03, IUS/04, IUS/05, IUS/06, IUS/07, IUS/08, IUS/09, IUS/10, IUS/12, IUS/13, IUS/14, IUS/15, IUS/16, IUS/17, IUS/20, IUS/21. At least 12 CFU must be obtained in the SSDs IUS/01 Private Law and IUS/08 or IUS/09 Constitutional or Public Law.
2) At least 12 CFU in the sectors SECS-P and SECS-S: SECS-P/01, SECS-P/02, SECS-P/03, SECS-P/04, SECS-P/05, SECS-P/06, SECS-P/07, SECS-P/08, SECS-P/09, SECS-P/10, SECS-P/11, SECS-P/13, SECS-S/01, SECS-S/03, SECS-S/05.
3) At least 12 CFU in the sectors: INF/01, ING-INF/05, ING-IND/35, SPS/04, SPS/07, SPS/09, SPS/12, M-STO/02, M-GGR/01, M-PSI/05, M-PSI/06, L-LIN.
Without prejudice to the above, enrollment is subject to verification of personal preparation and/or passing an admission test and possession of a level of English language proficiency of at least B2. In the verification of admission requirements, a tolerance of up to 10% is allowed on individual disciplinary groupings or on the total.
The admission requirements are considered to be met in the case of obtaining a three-year Bachelor's degree in the class L-14 or a single-cycle Master's degree in the class L-MG/01 with a final grade of over 90/110.
In other cases, the possession of the curricular requirements will be ascertained, according to the methods defined in the Didactic Regulations of the Course of Study and on the basis of the documentation provided by the candidate, by a Commission appointed by the Course Council, which will verify the student's personal preparation through an oral interview and/or the administration of an admission test. The Commission will inform the students of the readings to be carried out in order to improve their basic knowledge and will set a calendar for the interviews, published on the institutional website of the Course of Study and the Department of Legal and Social Sciences. At the end of the interview, if the Commission considers the level of knowledge and skills of the student to be sufficient, it will express a positive opinion on admission. In this case, the student has the right to enroll in the Course of Study. In case of a negative outcome, the Commission will justify the non-admission, making the student's enrollment conditional on making up for the missing credits, by passing the relevant exams or, for linguistic deficiencies, obtaining adequate certification.

UNIVERSITY: UNIVERSITÀ DEGLI STUDI GABRIELE D'ANNUNZIO – CHIETI/PESCARA
COURSE: [687M] PSYCHOLOGY OF WELL-BEING AND PERFORMANCE
TYPE: Laurea Magistrale
SECTION: INFO PIANI DI STUDIO
URL: https://unich.coursecatalogue.cineca.it/corsi/2024/2305

INFO PIANI DI STUDIO General information regarding the current academic year. Duration: 2 years. Program: 2023. Status: Open. Credits: 120 CFU. Language: English. Course Type: Laurea Magistrale. Regulations: Reform 270/04. Degree Class: LM-51 - Class of Master's Degrees in Psychology. Inter-university Course: No. Open Admission: Yes. Location: CHIETI (Sustainable capacity: 12). Department: DEPARTMENT OF PSYCHOLOGY. Website: https://www.unich.it/didattica/iscrizioni. Admission Requirements: Required knowledge for admission. To be admitted to the Master's Degree course in Psychology of well-being and performance, applicants must hold a Bachelor's degree or a three-year university diploma, or another equivalent degree obtained abroad. Admission to the course is conditional upon possessing: For Italian students: a degree in the L-24 SCIENZE E TECNICHE PSICOLOGICHE (former D.M. 270/04) class, or a degree in the 34 SCIENZE E TECNICHE PSICOLOGICHE (former D.M. 509/99) class, or possess a degree and have obtained at least 60 CFU in the field of psychology. For students from foreign universities: solid basic knowledge in all areas of psychology: the psychophysiological processes underlying behavior, general psychology, social psychology, developmental psychology, clinical psychology, the dynamics of human relationships, methods of psychological investigation, statistical methods, psychometric methods, and computer procedures for data processing. b. knowledge of the English language at least at the B2 level of the Common European Framework of Reference. Verification of personal preparation is mandatory, and only students who meet the curricular requirements can be admitted. The adequacy of personal preparation is verified by a Commission composed of the course lecturers. For the purpose of enrollment in the Master's Degree course in Psychology, those who have obtained a degree in Scienze e tecniche psicologiche - class L-24 according to the previous regulations and who have not completed the professional training activities corresponding to the 10 CFU referred to in paragraph 5 (Interministerial Decree no. 654 of 5-7-2022), may request recognition of activities carried out and certified during the three-year degree course, with regard to: a) activities, carried out individually or in small groups, aimed at learning methods, tools, and procedures related to the application contexts of psychology; b) practical experiences, laboratories, simulations, role-playing, drafting of projects, carried out individually or in small groups, concerning tools, methods, and procedures aimed at developing the knowledge necessary to operate in the contexts in which psychologists intervene. In the absence of, or partial recognition of, the aforementioned CFU (Interministerial Decree no. 654 of 5-7-2022, art. 2, paragraph 6), three-year degree graduates will acquire the missing internship CFU in addition to the 120 CFU of the Master's degree.

Admission requirements: Required knowledge for admission. Students with a degree in the following classes (or those with an equivalent degree obtained abroad and recognized as valid) can access the Master's degree program:
L-8 INFORMATION ENGINEERING
L-9 INDUSTRIAL ENGINEERING
L-18 ECONOMICS AND BUSINESS MANAGEMENT
L-24 PSYCHOLOGICAL SCIENCES AND TECHNIQUES
L-30 PHYSICS
L-31 COMPUTER SCIENCE
L-35 MATHEMATICS
L-41 STATISTICS
L-SNT/01 HEALTH, NURSING AND OBSTETRIC HEALTH PROFESSIONS
L-SNT/02 REHABILITATION HEALTH PROFESSIONS
L-SNT/03 TECHNICAL HEALTH PROFESSIONS
L-SNT/04 PREVENTION HEALTH PROFESSIONS
Students with a degree in other degree classes can also access the program, provided they demonstrate that they have at least 30 total training credits in one or more of the following groups of scientific-disciplinary sectors (SSD):
First group: M/PSI-01, M/PSI-02, M/PSI-03, M/PSI-04, M/PSI-05, M/PSI-06, M/PSI-07, M/PSI-08;
Second group: SECS-P/07; SECS-P/08; SECS-P/10; SECS-S/01; SECS-S/02; SECS-S/03; SECS-S/04; SECS-S/05; SECS-S/06;
Third group: INF/01; ING-INF/05; MAT/01; MAT/02; MAT/03; MAT/04; MAT/05; MAT/06; MAT/07; MAT/08; MAT/09;
Fourth group: BIO/09; BIO/14; MED/01; MED/03; MED/09; MED/25; MED/26; MED/42; MED/45; MED/48; MED/50
For admission, a minimum curricular requirement related to knowledge of the English language is also required, equal to a B2 level or higher. For foreign students, knowledge of Italian is required, equal to a B2 level or higher. The methods for verifying knowledge of the English or Italian language will be defined in the Course Regulations.
To attend the Master's Degree Course in Computational Cognitive Sciences profitably, familiarity with basic concepts of psychophysiological processes in humans, knowledge of statistical or psychometric methods, and familiarity with computer procedures for data processing are necessary.

UNIVERSITY: UNIVERSITÀ DEGLI STUDI GABRIELE D'ANNUNZIO – CHIETI/PESCARA
COURSE: [700M] ARCHITECTURE
TYPE: Master's Degree
CURRICULUM: Single-Cycle Program, 5 years
SECTION: COURSE STRUCTURE INFORMATION
URL: https://unich.coursecatalogue.cineca.it/corsi/2024/8

COURSE STRUCTURE INFORMATION General information for the current academic year. Duration: 5 years. Program start: 2019. Status: Open. Credits: 300 CFU. Language: Italian. Course type: Master's Degree Courses. Regulations: Reform 270/04. Degree class: LM-4 c.u. - Class of Master's Degrees in Architecture and Building and Architectural Engineering (five-year program). Inter-university course: No. Limited access (125 places). Other locations: PESCARA (Sustainable capacity: 125). Department: DEPARTMENT OF ARCHITECTURE. Website: http://www.dda.unich.it/didattica/laurea-architettura-lm-4. Teaching method: Conventional. Verification of knowledge required for access: Yes. Course lecturers: View list. Admission requirements: Knowledge required for access. To enroll in the Master's Degree program, a five-year upper secondary school diploma is required. Enrollment in the single-cycle Master's Degree program in Architecture is subject to passing an entrance test according to current regulations. The test includes knowledge of general culture and logical reasoning, as well as basic knowledge in the following subjects: history of architecture, drawing, physics, and mathematics. The ranking of the entrance test also serves as proof of verification of the knowledge required for the assignment of any Additional Training Obligations (ATO). The ATOs are divided into the same thematic areas (Mathematics and Physics, Drawing and Representation, History of Architecture), consist of the assignment of individual training activities, and are assigned based on the failure to reach the minimum score threshold for each area in the entrance test, as established by the Course Regulations.

UNIVERSITY: GABRIELE D'ANNUNZIO UNIVERSITY OF CHIETI/PESCARA
COURSE: [707T] DESIGN
SECTION: COURSE PLAN INFORMATION
URL: https://unich.coursecatalogue.cineca.it/corsi/2024/1083

COURSE PLAN INFORMATION General information regarding the current academic year. Duration: 3 years. Program: 2016. Status: Open. Credits: 180 CFU. Language: Italian. Course Type: Degree Course. Regulatory Reform: 270/04. Degree Class: L-4 - Class of Degrees in Industrial Design. Inter-University Course: No. Programmed Access (80 places). Other locations: PESCARA (Sustainable capacity: 80). Department: DEPARTMENT OF ARCHITECTURE. Website: https://www.dda.unich.it/didattica/laurea-design-l-4. Teaching method: Conventional. Knowledge verification required for access: Yes. Course lecturers: View list. Admission requirements: Knowledge required for access. To enroll in the Degree Course in Design, applicants must possess a high school diploma obtained in Italy or another equivalent qualification obtained abroad. Applicants must also possess a good knowledge of spoken and written Italian, good logical reasoning skills, and adequate basic knowledge of mathematics, art history, architecture, and design, drawing and representation, and English. The methods for verifying this knowledge and skills will be determined in the course's didactic regulations. If the verification of the knowledge required for access is not positive, the didactic regulations of the course will also indicate any additional training obligations that must be completed in the first year of the course. Admission procedure: The Degree Course in Design has programmed access and requires a mandatory entrance exam, which will be held online, via the 'Cineca QUID from home' platform. For the academic year 2024/2025, there are 80 places available for admission to the Degree Course in Design, of which: - 75 places are reserved for EU students, as well as non-EU students residing in Italy. - 5 places are reserved for non-EU students residing abroad, with the possibility of reintegrating EU and Italian students if no suitable non-EU students are found. The selection of candidates and the merit ranking are based on the following evaluation elements: - result of the test - academic record (final exam grade) The test consists of a series of multiple-choice questions with four possible answers, only one of which is correct. The test consists of 40 questions divided into 5 sections to verify the candidates' knowledge in the following subject areas: - Mathematical analysis and Geometry (5 questions) - Drawing and Representation (5 questions) - History of art and design (10 questions) - Logic (5 questions) - General culture and verbal comprehension (15 questions) The points awarded for each answer are: - correct answer: 1 (one) - unanswered or multiple answer: 0 (zero) - incorrect answer: - 0.25 (minus zero point twenty-five) The evaluation of the academic record, which is added to the score obtained in the test, considers the grade obtained in the final exam. The conversion of the final exam grade, obtained by July 2024 and valid for enrollment in a first-level degree course, is obtained according to the following table: - for grades from 70 to 74: 1 point - for grades from 75 to 79: 2 points - for grades from 80 to 84: 4 points - for grades from 85 to 89: 6 points - for grades from 90 to 94: 8 points - for grades from 95 to 100: 10 points - for a grade of 100 with honors: 12 points The merit score of the entrance test also serves as a verification of the knowledge required for access and for the assignment of any additional training obligations (ATO). The ATOs are assigned based on the failure to achieve a minimum score in the following subject areas: Mathematical analysis and Geometry: minimum score of 1.5 out of a maximum of 5 points. Drawing and Representation: minimum score of 1.5 out of a maximum of 5 points. History of art and design: minimum score of 3 out of a maximum of 10 points. The ATOs will be fulfilled by participating in individual training activities assigned directly by the reference lecturer or by participating in supplementary teaching courses held by tutors with expertise in the subject areas of the ATOs, or through one-on-one in-depth tutoring within the reference courses. In all cases, a final evaluation is provided through a test or interview with the reference lecturer. The deadline for completing the ATOs assigned in the subject areas of Geometry, Drawing and Representation, and History of Art and Design is set for October 31, 2025. The ATOs assigned in one or more subject areas are considered to be fulfilled with the passing of the exams in the 1st year of: - Geometry for Design for the subject area of Mathematical Analysis and Geometry - Drawing for Design for the subject area of Drawing and Representation - History of Design for the subject area of History of Art and Design. Enrollment in the second year is allowed, but it is not possible to take exams in the second year before completing any assigned ATOs. For students who enroll directly in the 2nd year, the ATOs are considered to be fulfilled with the achievement of at least 36 CFU in the TAF AeB courses.

Admission requirements: Required knowledge for admission. The knowledge required for admission to the Master's Degree course in Digital Marketing concerns the following three areas:
1. Minimum curricular requirements
2. Adequate personal preparation
3. Adequate knowledge of the English language
1. Minimum curricular requirements.
In order to be admitted to the course, applicants must hold a first-level degree or an equivalent foreign qualification.
The following students may enroll in the Master's Degree course in Digital Marketing:
- Holders of a first-level degree in one of the following classes (ex DM 270/04 or equivalent based on the old system or an equivalent qualification obtained at a foreign University):
L-08 - Degrees in Information Engineering
L-09 - Degrees in Industrial Engineering
L-11 - Degrees in Languages and Foreign Literatures
L-12 - Degrees in Linguistic Mediation
L-15 - Degrees in Tourism Sciences
L-16 - Degrees in Business and Organizational Sciences
L-18 - Degrees in Economics and Business Management Sciences
L-20 - Degrees in Communication Sciences
L-33 - Degrees in Economic Sciences
L-41 - Degrees in Statistics
- Holders of a second-level degree in one of the following classes: Cl. 64S and Cl. 84S ex DM509/99, LM-56 and LM-77 ex DM270/04 or an equivalent qualification obtained at a foreign University;
- Holders of a four-year degree from the old system (pre DM509/99) in Economics, obtained at an Italian University or an equivalent qualification obtained at a foreign University;
- Holders of a degree other than those mentioned above, provided they have at least 48 CFU distributed as follows:
Business area: 24 CFU (SECS-P/07, SECS-P/08, SECS-P/09 SECS P/10, SECS P/11)
Economic area: 12 CFU (SECS-P/01, SECS-P/02, SECS-P/03, SECS-P/05, SECS-P/06)
Quantitative-informatics area: 12 CFU (from SECS-S/01 to SECS-S/06, from MAT/01 to MAT/09, INF/01 and ING-INF/05)
2. Adequate personal preparation.
Personal preparation is considered adequate if it is based on knowledge of the basic topics included in the following areas of learning: economics, business, statistics-mathematics and law. The verification of the adequacy of personal preparation will take place according to the methods provided for in the didactic regulations of the course.
3. Adequate knowledge of the English language.
For admission purposes, knowledge of the English language, in written and oral form, at least at level B2 is also required. The verification of the adequacy of knowledge of the English language will take place according to the methods provided for in the didactic regulations of the course.

Admission requirements: Required knowledge for admission. To be admitted to the Master's degree program, applicants must hold a Bachelor's or three-year university degree, or another equivalent degree obtained abroad, and must have acquired the curricular requirements that include adequate knowledge of general scientific methods and content in the basic scientific disciplines and in the disciplines of Science and Technology of building (class L-23), which are preparatory to the specific subjects provided for in the curriculum of the Master's degree class LM-24 (Engineering of building systems).
For admission to the Course of Study, it is an indispensable curricular requirement to have acquired, with reference to what is defined for the ministerial class L-23, at least: 24 CFU in Basic training activities in the disciplinary area 'Basic scientific training', 12 CFU in Basic training activities in the disciplinary area 'Basic training in history and representation', 45 CFU in Specific training activities in the disciplinary areas 'Architecture and urban planning', 'Building and environment' and 'Engineering for safety and protection of building structures'. In addition, knowledge of the English language is required at least at level B2 (according to the Common European Framework of Reference for Languages).
The curricular requirements must be met by candidates before the assessment of the adequacy of their personal preparation, which will take place according to the methods indicated in the didactic regulations of the course of study.

Admission requirements: Required knowledge for admission. To be admitted to the L23 Bachelor's Degree in Civil Engineering, applicants must hold a high school diploma obtained in Italy or another equivalent qualification obtained abroad that is recognized as suitable.

UNIVERSITY: UNIVERSITY OF STUDIES GABRIELE D'ANNUNZIO – CHIETI/PESCARA
COURSE: [807M] ECO INCLUSIVE DESIGN
TYPE: Master's Degree
SECTION: INFO ON STUDY PLANS
URL: https://unich.coursecatalogue.cineca.it/corsi/2024/1884

INFO ON STUDY PLANS General information for the current academic year.
Duration: 2 years
Academic year: 2023
Status: Open
Credits: 120 CFU
Language: Italian
Course type: Master's Degree Courses
Regulation: 270/04 Reform
Degree class: LM-12 - Class of Master's Degrees in Design
Inter-university course: No
Open access: Yes
Other locations: PESCARA (Sustainable use: 8)
Department: DEPARTMENT OF ARCHITECTURE
Website: https://www.dda.unich.it/Laurea-Magistrale-in-Eco-Inclusive-Design-LM-12
Admission requirements: Required knowledge for admission.
To be admitted to the Master's Degree Course in Eco Inclusive Design, the following knowledge is required:
- drawing and traditional and digital representation.
- methodologies, processes, and techniques of design in the fields of product design, interior design, and communication design.
- history of design and Italian and international design culture.
- technological culture of basic materials and main production processes related to product design.
Curriculum requirements: To be admitted to the Master's Degree Course in Eco Inclusive Design, applicants must hold a Bachelor's degree (or a three-year university diploma) or another equivalent qualification obtained abroad, in the following classes:
- L-4 Industrial Design (and in the corresponding class 42, as per DM 509/99);
- L-17 (Architecture);
- L-21 (Territorial, urban, landscape, and environmental planning);
- L-23 (Construction science and technology);
or a Master's degree (or a five-year university diploma) or another equivalent qualification obtained abroad, in the following class:
- LM-4 (Architecture);
or a diploma in the following courses:
- DIPL01 - DIPL02 (First-level academic diploma of the Higher Institutes for Artistic Industries - ISIA)
- DAPL06 (First-level academic diploma of the Academies of Fine Arts)
For the degree classes L-17 (Architecture), L-21 (Territorial, urban, landscape, and environmental planning), L-23 (Construction science and technology), LM-4 (Architecture), and for the first-level academic diplomas DIPL01 - DIPL02 - DAPL06, it is also necessary to have acquired a minimum of 18 educational credits in the following scientific-disciplinary sectors or in equivalent courses offered by the ISIA Institutes and the Academies of Fine Arts:
- ICAR/12 Architectural technology;
- ICAR/13 Industrial design;
- ICAR/17 Drawing.
For Italian-speaking students, admission to the course is subject to documented ability to correctly use, in written and oral form, the English language, at least at the B2 level, according to the criteria defined in the didactic regulations. For other students, knowledge of the Italian language (certified B2 level) is required.
Verification of personal preparation is mandatory and only students who meet the curriculum requirements can access it. It involves an evaluation process of the applications submitted by the candidates, conducted by an Admission Committee, appointed by the Course Council and nominated by the Rector.

UNIVERSITY: UNIVERSITY OF STUDIES GABRIELE D'ANNUNZIO – CHIETI/PESCARA
COURSE: [839M] ECONOMICS AND COMMERCE
TYPE: Master's Degree
SECTION: INFO ON STUDY PLANS
URL: https://unich.coursecatalogue.cineca.it/corsi/2024/572

INFO ON STUDY PLANS General information for the current academic year. Duration: 2 years. Regulations: 2013. Status: Open. Credits: 120 CFU. Language: Italian. Course Type: Master's Degree Courses. Regulations: Reform 270/04. Degree Class: LM-56 - Class of Master's Degrees in Economics. Inter-university Course: No. Open Admission. Other locations: PESCARA (Sustainable capacity: 50). Department: DEPARTMENT OF ECONOMICS. Website: http://www.dec.unich.it/. Admission requirements: Required knowledge for admission. To be admitted to the Master's Degree Course in Economics and Commerce, applicants must hold a Bachelor's degree. Admission to the course is also subject to obtaining a specific number of CFU in specific subject areas (SSD), possibly including an assessment of the content. In particular, all those who have obtained the following educational credits in their Bachelor's degree (basic, characterizing, qualifying, or chosen by the student) may be admitted to the Master's Degree Course in Economics and Commerce: a) SECS-P/01; SECS-P/02; SECS-P/03; SECS-P/06; SECS-P/12; M-GGR/02 minimum 18 CFU b) SECS-P/07, SECS-P/08; SECS-P/09; SECS-P/10; SECS-P/11; SECS-P/13 minimum 18 CFU c) MAT/02; MAT/05; MAT/06; MAT/09 SECS-S/01; SECS-S/03; SECS S/06 minimum 18 CFU d) IUS/01; IUS/04; IUS/05; IUS 07; IUS/09; IUS/10, IUS/14; IUS/12; minimum 18 CFU. A tolerance of up to 10% (i.e., 9 CFU) is allowed. This margin of tolerance may apply to one or more of the aforementioned groups of SSDs. Knowledge, in written and oral form, of at least one language of the European Union, in addition to Italian, is also required. The verification of these requirements takes place through a specific and formalized verification process. In particular, each application for enrollment is examined by a special commission of the Course, which evaluates the individual academic careers of the students (if necessary, also through a verification of the teaching programs) in order to ascertain the existence of the admission requirements. Transfers, changes of course, and admission to individual exams are subject to approval by the Council. The Council may grant full or partial recognition of the academic career pursued up to that point, with the validation of exams taken and credits obtained, and indicates the year of the course to which the student is enrolled.

UNIVERSITY: UNIVERSITÀ DEGLI STUDI GABRIELE D'ANNUNZIO – CHIETI/PESCARA
COURSE: [910T] SCIENZE DELL'HABITAT SOSTENIBILE
SECTION: INFO PIANI DI STUDIO
URL: https://unich.coursecatalogue.cineca.it/corsi/2024/1864

INFO PIANI DI STUDIO General information regarding the current academic year. Duration: 3 years. Admission: 2024. Status: Open. Credits: 180 CFU. Language: Italian. Course Type: Laurea (Bachelor's Degree). Regulatory Framework: Reform 270/04. Degree Class: L-21 - Class of degrees in Territorial, urban, landscape and environmental planning sciences. Inter-university Course: No. Open Access: Yes. Other locations: PESCARA (Sustainable design: 37). Department: DIPARTIMENTO DI ARCHITETTURA. Website: https://www.dda.unich.it/didattica/laurea-scienze-habitat-sostenibile-l-21.
Admission Requirements: Required knowledge for admission. To be admitted to the Bachelor's Degree in Sustainable Habitat Sciences, applicants must hold a high school diploma or another qualification obtained abroad and deemed suitable. All students must take an assessment test to verify their basic knowledge. Students admitted to the course with a score below the predetermined minimum score will be assigned one or more additional learning requirements (OFA) in the subjects for which adequate basic knowledge is required: mathematics and physics, drawing-representation, and history of architecture. The assessment of the knowledge required for admission to the degree course will take place according to the methods determined annually in the Course Regulations.
Additional learning requirements: A negative result in the assessment test leads to the assignment of one or more additional learning requirements (OFA) in the subjects for which adequate basic knowledge is required. These OFAs must be completed during the first year of the course, based on criteria defined annually and specified in the Course Regulations.

[A595] PHARMACEUTICAL CHEMISTRY AND TECHNOLOGY Master's Degree, Single-Cycle Program, 5 years

CHEMISTRY OF NATURAL SUBSTANCES AND BIOTECHNOLOGICAL-PHARMACEUTICAL-INNOVATIVE FOODS, MATERIALS SCIENCE AND BIOMATERIALS, INDUSTRIAL AND PHARMACEUTICAL

-----

UNIVERSITY: UNIVERSITÀ DEGLI STUDI GABRIELE D'ANNUNZIO – CHIETI/PESCARA
COURSE: [A595] PHARMACEUTICAL CHEMISTRY AND TECHNOLOGY
TYPE: Master's Degree
CURRICULUM: Single-Cycle Program, 5 years
SECTION: COURSE STRUCTURE INFORMATION
URL: https://unich.coursecatalogue.cineca.it/corsi/2024/5110

COURSE STRUCTURE INFORMATION General information regarding the current academic year. Duration: 5 years. Admission year: 2023. Status: Open. Credits: 300 CFU. Language: Italian. Course type: Master's Degree. Regulations: 270/04 Reform. Degree class: LM-13 - Pharmacy and Pharmaceutical Technology. Inter-university course: No. Programmed access (100 places). Location: CHIETI (Sustainable capacity: 100). Department: DEPARTMENT OF PHARMACY. Website: https://www.farmacia.unich.it/ctf. Teaching method: Conventional. Verification of knowledge required for access: Yes. Course lecturers: View list. Admission requirements: Knowledge required for access. Students who intend to enroll in the Master's Degree Course in Pharmaceutical Chemistry and Technology are required to have knowledge of basic sciences, logical reasoning skills, and text comprehension skills, as provided by the educational programs of the upper secondary school. In particular, adequate initial preparation is required in the following subjects:
1. Mathematics (Proportions, percentages, radicals, powers, logarithms, equivalences. First-degree equations).
2. Physics (Physical quantities. Units and systems of measurement).
3. Chemistry (Periodic table of elements. Substances, elements, mixtures, and compounds. Concept of chemical reaction. Changes of state).
4. Biology (Knowledge of the cell. Basic knowledge of the main biological molecules).
Enrollment in the Master's Degree Course in Pharmaceutical Chemistry and Technology requires a five-year upper secondary school diploma or another equivalent degree obtained abroad that is recognized as suitable. The course has a programmed number of places, and an entrance exam is required for the selection of students to be admitted. The admission test consists of answering multiple-choice questions on topics of Chemistry, Biology, Physics, Mathematics, Logic, and English. Remedial courses will be provided within the first year of the course to address any additional training requirements (OFA) in the subjects of Mathematics, Physics, Biology, and Chemistry, which are covered in the admission test, for candidates who score below a certain threshold. The methods and date of the admission test will be adequately announced on the University and Department websites (www.farmacia.unich.it).

[A596] FARMACIA Laurea Magistrale Ciclo Unico 5 anni

PHARMACEUTICAL CARE, FUNCTIONAL COSMETICS, NUTRITION AND FOOD, EXPERIMENTAL AND PHARMACEUTICAL PHYTOPTHERAPY

-----

UNIVERSITY: UNIVERSITÀ DEGLI STUDI GABRIELE D'ANNUNZIO – CHIETI/PESCARA
COURSE: [A596] FARMACIA
TYPE: Laurea Magistrale
CURRICULUM: Ciclo Unico 5 anni
SECTION: INFO PIANI DI STUDIO
URL: https://unich.coursecatalogue.cineca.it/corsi/2024/5111

INFO PIANI DI STUDIO General information regarding the current academic year. Duration: 5 years. Program: 2023. Status: Open. Credits: 300 CFU. Language: Italian. Course Type: Laurea Magistrale. Regulations: Reform 270/04. Degree Class: LM-13 - Pharmacy and Pharmaceutical Technology. Inter-university Course: No. Programmed Access (160 places). Location: CHIETI (Sustainable capacity: 160). Department: DIPARTIMENTO DI FARMACIA. Website: https://www.farmacia.unich.it/farmacia. Teaching Method: Conventional. Verification of knowledge required for access: Yes. Course Lecturers: View list. Admission Requirements: Knowledge required for access. Students intending to enroll in the Laurea Magistrale Degree in Pharmacy must have knowledge of basic sciences, logical reasoning skills, and text comprehension skills, as provided by the educational programs of secondary schools. In particular, adequate initial preparation is required in the following subjects: 1. Mathematics (Proportions, percentages, radicals, powers, logarithms, equivalences. First-degree equations). 2. Physics (Physical quantities. Units and measurement systems). 3. Chemistry (Periodic table of elements. Substances, elements, mixtures, and compounds. Concept of chemical reaction. Changes of state). 4. Biology (Knowledge of the cell. Basic knowledge of the main biological molecules). Enrollment in the Laurea Magistrale Degree in Pharmacy requires a five-year secondary school diploma or another equivalent degree obtained abroad that is recognized as valid. The course has a limited number of places, and prospective students are selected based on a merit ranking drawn up according to the diploma grade from secondary school, and, secondarily, takes into account the chronological order of pre-registration for the ranking, according to the methods established by a specific announcement. Following enrollment, additional training obligations (OFA) will be defined through the administration of multiple-choice questions prepared by the lecturers, containing questions on topics of Chemistry, Biology, Physics, and Mathematics. Any recovery of the OFA must take place within the first year of the course.

UNIVERSITY: UNIVERSITÀ DEGLI STUDI GABRIELE D'ANNUNZIO – CHIETI/PESCARA
COURSE: [BASA] BENI ARCHEOLOGICI E STORICO-ARTISTICI
TYPE: Laurea Magistrale
SECTION: INFO PIANI DI STUDIO
URL: https://unich.coursecatalogue.cineca.it/corsi/2024/547

INFO PIANI DI STUDIO General information regarding the current academic year. Duration: 2 years. Academic year: 2024. Status: Open. Credits: 120 CFU. Language: Italian. Course type: Laurea Magistrale. Regulations: Reform 270/04. Degree class: LM-2 - Degree class in Archeology. Interclass degree: LM-89 - Degree class in History of Art. Inter-university course: No. Open access: Yes. Location: CHIETI (Sustainable capacity: 30). Department: DIPARTIMENTO DI LETTERE, ARTI E SCIENZE SOCIALI. Admission requirements: Required knowledge for admission. Those who hold a Bachelor's degree in Cultural Heritage (class L-1 according to DM 270), class CI.13 (Cultural Heritage Sciences according to DM 509), Diploma in Cultural Heritage Operator (ex L. 341/90), class CI.41 (Technologies for the conservation and restoration of cultural heritage according to DM 509), class L-43 (Technologies for the conservation and restoration of cultural heritage according to DM 270) and graduates in class CI.23 (Sciences and technologies of visual arts, music, performing arts and fashion according to DM 509), class L-3 (Sciences and technologies of visual arts, music, performing arts and fashion according to DM 270 and equivalent diplomas from the Academy of Fine Arts) and class L-10 (Letters according to DM 270}, class CI.5 (Letters according to DM 509) may access the Master's degree program. Graduates from other degree programs, even with the old system, may also access the Master's degree program, provided that they have obtained at least 54 CFU in total in the scientific-disciplinary sectors provided for in the Bachelor's degree program in Cultural Heritage (all L-ART*, all L-ANT*, all L-FIL LET*, the L-OR from /01 to /06, L-LIN/01, M-GGR/01, all M-STO*, IUS/10, IUS/18, ICAR/06, ICAR/15, ICAR 18 E ICAR 19, ICAR/21, GEO/01, GEO/07, GEO/02, GEO/10, BIO/08, M DEA/01, FIS/07, SPS/08, SPS/10, CHIM/12). Furthermore, the ability to use fluently, in written and oral form, at least one language of the European Union in addition to Italian, also with reference to disciplinary lexicons, through adequate certification (language proficiency acquired in the Bachelor's degree or European certificate, equivalent to the B1 level of English) is required. For all students, access is conditional on possessing the curricular requirements set forth in the regulations and having adequate personal preparation.

UNIVERSITY: UNIVERSITÀ DEGLI STUDI GABRIELE D'ANNUNZIO – CHIETI/PESCARA
COURSE: [FLT7] FILOLOGY, LINGUISTICS AND LITERARY TRADITIONS
TYPE: Laurea Magistrale
SECTION: INFO PIANI DI STUDIO
URL: https://unich.coursecatalogue.cineca.it/corsi/2024/61

INFO PIANI DI STUDIO General information regarding the current academic year. Duration: 2 years. Degree program: 2018. Status: Open. Credits: 120 CFU. Language: Italian. Course type: Laurea Magistrale. Regulations: Reform 270/04. Degree class: LM-14 - Degree class in Modern Philology. Interclass degree: LM-15 - Degree class in Philology, Literatures and History of Antiquity. Inter-university course: No. Open access: Yes. Location: CHIETI (Sustainable capacity: 84). Department: DIPARTIMENTO DI LETTERE, ARTI E SCIENZE SOCIALI. Admission requirements: Required knowledge for admission: To access the course, it is necessary to have a Bachelor's degree in Literature (class L-10), or an equivalent degree (Italian or foreign), or a degree obtained in another class with a minimum of 60 CFU acquired among the following SSDs: L-FIL-LET/02, L-FIL-LET/04, L-FIL-LET/05, L-FIL-LET/06, L-FIL-LET/07, L-FIL-LET/08, L-FIL-LET/09, L-FIL-LET/10, L-FIL-LET/11, L-FIL-LET/12, L-FIL-LET/13, L-FIL-LET/14, L-LIN/01, L-LIN/02 L-ANT/02, L-ANT/03, M-STO/01, M-STO/02, M-STO/03, M-STO/04, M-STO/05, M-STO/08, M-STO/09. Admission to the Master's degree course is subject to passing a verification of the adequacy of personal preparation, which will take place according to the methods defined in the admission methods section. Adequate language skills in a language of the European Union (level B1) will also be verified.
Admission methods: Admission to the course is subject to a mandatory evaluation of the student's curriculum by the Pre-evaluation Commission. The Commission verifies the possession of the curricular requirements and, in particular, the possession of the 60 CFU acquired in the SSDs necessary for admission: L-FIL-LET/02, L-FIL-LET/04, L-FIL-LET/05, L-FIL-LET/06, L-FIL-LET/07, L-FIL-LET/08, L-FIL-LET/09, L-FIL-LET/10, L-FIL-LET/11, L-FIL-LET/12, L-FIL-LET/13, L-FIL-LET/14, L-LIN/01, L-LIN/02 L-ANT/02, L-ANT/03, M-STO/01, M-STO/02, M-STO/03, M-STO/04, M-STO/05, M-STO/08, M-STO/09. The Commission communicates the result of the evaluation to the candidate and, in case of a negative outcome, the methods for obtaining any missing CFUs. With reference to the verification of knowledge and skills in a language of the European Union, this can be considered fulfilled by passing a language proficiency test in the previous educational path at level B1.
