UNIVERSITY: UNIVERSITÀ DEGLI STUDI GABRIELE D'ANNUNZIO – CHIETI/PESCARA
COURSE: [L018] ECONOMICS AND MANAGEMENT
SECTION: INFO ON STUDY PLANS
URL: https://unich.coursecatalogue.cineca.it/corsi/2024/573

INFO ON STUDY PLANS General information regarding the current academic year.
Duration: 3 years.
Program start year: 2019.
Status: Open.
Credits: 180 CFU.
Language: Italian.
Course type: Bachelor's Degree.
Regulation: 270/04 reform.
Degree class: L-18 - Class of degrees in Economics and Business Management.
Inter-university course: No.
Open access: Yes.
Other locations: PESCARA (Sustainable capacity: 339).
Department: DEPARTMENT OF BUSINESS ECONOMICS.
Website: https://clem.unich.it/visualizza.php?type=gruppo&id=2225
Admission requirements: Required knowledge for admission. To be admitted to the Bachelor's Degree program, applicants must hold a five-year secondary school diploma or an equivalent qualification obtained abroad and recognized as valid, or a four-year secondary school diploma and the related supplementary year.
For admission to the Economics and Management Bachelor's Degree program, the following are also required:
- verbal and linguistic skills related to the comprehension, processing, and presentation of texts in Italian;
- basic knowledge of the English language;
- a general culture that allows for understanding socio-economic phenomena;
- logical and deductive skills;
- basic mathematical and computer skills.
As provided for in Article 6 of Decree 270/2004, the verification of these knowledge and skills takes place through an entrance test, which is carried out according to the criteria established annually by the University and indicated on the course website. The verification test aims to evaluate the initial preparation of students in the following areas: ability to understand a written text; mathematics; logic.
In case of failure to pass or complete the test, the student will be assigned an additional training requirement (OFA), to be completed within the first year of the course, according to the methods established by the course's didactic regulations.

UNIVERSITY: UNIVERSITÀ DEGLI STUDI GABRIELE D'ANNUNZIO – CHIETI/PESCARA
COURSE: [L022] SCIENZE DELLE ATTIVITÀ MOTORIE E SPORTIVE
SECTION: INFO PIANI DI STUDIO
URL: https://unich.coursecatalogue.cineca.it/corsi/2024/546

INFO PIANI DI STUDIO General information regarding the current academic year. Duration: 3 years. Program start year: 2016. Status: Open. Credits: 180 CFU. Language: Italian. Course type: Laurea (Bachelor's Degree). Regulatory framework: 270/04 reform. Degree class: L-22 - Class of degrees in Science of motor and sports activities. Inter-university course: No. Limited access (430 places). Location: CHIETI (Sustainable capacity: 430). Department: DIPARTIMENTO DI MEDICINA E SCIENZE DELL'INVECCHIAMENTO. Website: https://www.med.unich.it/corsi-di-studio/scienze-delle-attivita-motorie-e-sportive. Teaching method: Conventional. Verification of knowledge required for access: Yes. Course lecturers: View list. Admission requirements: Upper secondary school diploma or equivalent foreign diploma recognized according to regulations. In addition to the qualification required for admission to the degree course, students must have advanced-level knowledge and skills in logic, physics, chemistry, and biology. This knowledge will be verified, possibly concurrently with the selection test for admission. The content and methods for verifying the student's personal preparation are defined annually by the Course Council. If the verification is not positive, interested students will agree with the lecturers of the subjects to be recovered on the activities necessary to fill the gaps in their education. These activities will consist, alternatively, of additional lectures or the preparation of monographs on topics that will be defined by the lecturers based on the degree of deficiency reported by the student.

UNIVERSITY: UNIVERSITÀ DEGLI STUDI GABRIELE D'ANNUNZIO – CHIETI/PESCARA
COURSE: [L270] LETTERE
SECTION: INFO PIANI DI STUDIO
URL: https://unich.coursecatalogue.cineca.it/corsi/2024/62

INFO PIANI DI STUDIO General information regarding the current academic year. Duration: 3 years. Admission: 2019. Status: Open. Credits: 180 CFU. Language: Italian. Course Type: Laurea (Bachelor's Degree). Regulatory Framework: Reform 270/04. Degree Class: L-10 - Class of Degrees in Literature. Inter-University Course: No. Open Admission: Yes. Location: CHIETI (Sustainable capacity: 202). Department: DIPARTIMENTO DI LETTERE, ARTI E SCIENZE SOCIALI. Website: https://www.dilass.unich.it/didattica. Admission Requirements: Required knowledge for admission. Students with a high school diploma from Italian or foreign schools can enroll in the course (subject to verification of the equivalence of the degree and proficiency in the Italian language). In general, the following basic knowledge and skills are required:
- good general knowledge in the field of literary, linguistic, geographical, and historical disciplines;
- good logical and reasoning skills;
- good reading, comprehension, and interpretation skills of texts and documents (in Italian);
- good written and oral expression skills in Italian;
- A2 level knowledge of a European language (French, English, Spanish, German).
In the initial phase of the teaching activity, as required by Decree 270/2004, mandatory tests will be conducted to verify the aforementioned skills, specifically aimed at evaluating preparation in the linguistic, literary, historical, and geographical fields. The methods for verifying the required knowledge for admission will be specifically defined in the didactic regulations, as will the Additional Training Obligations (OFA) assigned in case of deficiencies in basic training.

UNIVERSITY: UNIVERSITÀ DEGLI STUDI GABRIELE D'ANNUNZIO – CHIETI/PESCARA
COURSE: [L460] INGEGNERIA BIOMEDICA
SECTION: INFO PIANI DI STUDIO
URL: https://unich.coursecatalogue.cineca.it/corsi/2024/1863

INFO PIANI DI STUDIO General information regarding the current academic year. Duration: 3 years. Admission: 2024. Status: Open. Credits: 180 CFU. Language: Italian. Course Type: Laurea (Bachelor's Degree). Regulations: Riforma 270/04. Degree Class: L-9 - Class of Industrial Engineering Degrees. Inter-university Course: No. Open Access: Yes. Other Locations: PESCARA (Sustainable capacity: 162). Department: DIPARTIMENTO DI INGEGNERIA E GEOLOGIA. Website: https://ibl9.unich.it/home-ibl9-18391.
Admission Requirements: Required knowledge for admission. To be admitted to the Bachelor's Degree program, applicants must hold a high school diploma or an equivalent qualification obtained abroad, recognized as suitable. In addition, the following are required: a good knowledge of the Italian language, logical reasoning skills, and knowledge and ability to use the main results of elementary mathematics and the fundamentals of experimental sciences. Adequate initial preparation is verified according to the methods described in the Didactic Regulations of the Courses of Study; if this verification is not positive, as can be ascertained through the evaluation of the CISIA tests, specific additional training obligations will be assigned to be completed within the first year of the course.

UNIVERSITY: UNIVERSITY OF STUDIES GABRIELE D'ANNUNZIO – CHIETI/PESCARA
COURSE: [L500] ECO-SUSTAINABLE TECHNOLOGIES AND ENVIRONMENTAL TOXICOLOGY
SECTION: INFORMATION ON STUDY PLANS
URL: https://unich.coursecatalogue.cineca.it/corsi/2024/1865

INFORMATION ON STUDY PLANS General information relating to the current academic year. Duration: 3 years. Admission: 2020. Status: Open. Credits: 180 CFU. Language: Italian. Course Type: Bachelor's Degree. Regulatory Reform: 270/04. Degree Class: L-29 - Class of degrees in Pharmaceutical Sciences and Technologies. Inter-university Course: No. Programmed Access (120 places). Location: CHIETI (Sustainable capacity: 120). Department: DEPARTMENT OF PHARMACY. Website: https://www.farmacia.unich.it/testa. Teaching Method: Conventional. Verification of knowledge required for access: Yes. Course Lecturers: View list. Admission Requirements: Required knowledge for access. Students must possess a high school diploma obtained in Italy or another qualification obtained abroad, recognized as suitable according to current regulations.
Access requires basic knowledge to be able to follow the degree course effectively. This knowledge includes a satisfactory familiarity with basic mathematical calculations, mastery of the main laws of mechanical physics, and basic knowledge of cellular biology and general chemistry, logical skills, an ability to express oneself orally and in writing without hesitation or errors, and a reasonable general culture.
The methods for verifying this knowledge and skills will be determined in the didactic regulations of the course of study. In the event that the verification of the knowledge required for access is not positive, the didactic regulations of the course of study will also indicate the additional training obligations that must be acquired in the first year of the course.
Admission Procedures: The Degree Course has a limited number of places. To enroll in this Degree Course, a high school diploma of five years' duration is required.
By the 15th of each year, the Course Council proposes to the Department Council the maximum number of students to be enrolled in the first year, as well as the terms for enrollment and transfers to be included in the annual study plan.
For the academic year 2024-25, the programmed student capacity is 120, of which 118 are for Italian, EU, and non-EU citizens residing in Italy, and two for non-EU citizens residing abroad (Art. 26, Law 189 of 30.07.02). In the event that the number of 120 is exceeded, a merit ranking will be drawn up based on the high school diploma grade, which will also take into account the chronological order of registration (in the event of equal grades, the chronological order of application submission will apply).
The admission procedures will be adequately publicized on the University website (https://www.unich.it) and on the notice boards of the teaching structure.
The terms for enrollment and transfers are published in the Study Plan and in the notice for admission to the Degree Course.
Additional Training Obligations (OFA): criteria for their determination and methods for recovery. After enrollment, the additional training obligations (OFA) will be defined by administering multiple-choice questions prepared by the lecturers, containing 80 questions on topics of Chemistry, Biology, Physics, Mathematics, and Logic.
The minimum diploma grade threshold for enrollment with exemption from the OFA is 80/100. Students who are included in the ranking with a diploma grade lower than 80/100 will have to take the test to verify the initial knowledge. Any recovery of the OFA must take place within the first year of the course and concerns the subjects of Mathematics, Physics, Biology, and Chemistry.
Methods for verifying the required knowledge: The test to verify the initial knowledge (mandatory pursuant to DM 270/04) is carried out in order to identify any training deficiencies of the enrolled students and to organize the necessary recovery activities (in addition to the activities of the ordinary teaching of the Degree Course), so as to guarantee didactic support to the students to whom, based on the deficiencies identified through the test of initial knowledge, OFA will be assigned.
For the purposes of verifying the initial knowledge, students transferring in, transferring courses, reactivating their studies, and enrolling in subsequent years with an abbreviated course will be excluded. All information relating to the test to verify the initial knowledge (date, time, location, number of questions, duration of the test, registration methods, methods for assigning the score to each correct, incorrect, or unanswered answer, etc.) will be published in the Admission Notice, on the University website (https://www.unich.it), and on the notice boards of the teaching structure.
In particular, the questions for the assignment of the OFA relate to the following disciplines:
- Physics:
Direct and indirect measurements. Fundamental and derived quantities. Physical dimensions of quantities. Decimal metric system. International System of Units (SI). Units of measure (names and relationships between fundamental and derived units). Multiples and submultiples. Kinematic quantities. Uniform rectilinear motion. Uniformly accelerated rectilinear motion. Uniform circular motion. Harmonic motion. Vectors and operations on vectors. Forces, moments of forces. Vector composition of forces. Definitions of mass and weight. Acceleration of gravity. Density and specific weight. Law of universal gravitation. Work. Kinetic energy. Potential energy. Pressure and its units of measure. Archimedes' principle. Mechanisms of heat propagation. Laws of perfect gases. Changes of state. Brief notes on acoustic and optical phenomena (reflection, refraction, dispersion). Electrostatics and electrodynamics. Electric field and potential. Electrical resistance and resistivity. Electrical work and power. Effects of electric currents.
- Mathematics:
Natural, integer, rational, and real numbers and their ordering and comparison. Algebraic operations and their properties. Proportions and percentages. Powers and their properties. Scientific notation. Radicals and their properties. Logarithms (in base 10 and in base e) and their properties. Algebraic expressions. Algebraic equations of the first and second degree. Inequalities. Fundamental notions on functions and their graphical representation. Measures of lengths, areas, and volumes. Measurement of angles in degrees and radians. Sine, cosine, tangent of an angle and their notable values. Cartesian reference system in the plane. Equation of the line. Conditions of parallelism and perpendicularity. Distance of a point from a line. Equation of the circle, parabola, hyperbola, ellipse and their representation in the Cartesian plane.
- Chemistry:
States of aggregation of matter. Heterogeneous and homogeneous systems. Compounds and elements. Ionic and molecular compounds. The composition of the atom (electrons, neutrons, protons). Atomic number and mass number. Atomic weight and molecular weight. Chemical reactions and stoichiometry (balancing and elementary stoichiometric calculations). Concept of mole. Avogadro's number. Solutions. Concentration of solutions. Concepts of acid and base. Acidity, neutrality, basicity of aqueous solutions. pH. Glycids. Lipids. Amino acids and proteins. Nucleic acids.
- Biology:
Organic molecules present in living organisms and their respective functions. Prokaryotic and eukaryotic cells. Animal and plant cells. Cell membrane and its functions. Cellular structures and their function. Cell division: mitosis and meiosis. Chromosome set. Animal and plant tissues. Photosynthesis. Glycolysis. Aerobic respiration. Fermentation. Sexual and asexual reproduction. Genes and DNA. Genetic code and its translation. Protein synthesis. Anatomy of the main systems and their respective functions and interactions. General notions on viruses, bacteria, and fungi. Main organs and systems of plants and their function.
In particular, as regards the method of verifying the possession of this knowledge, the test, to which a maximum time of 120 minutes will be assigned, consists of answering 80 multiple-choice questions (4 answers) and is structured as follows:
- 20 questions on chemistry
- 20 questions on physics
- 20 questions on mathematics
- 20 questions on biology. How are the Additional Training Obligations determined? The test to verify the initial knowledge will be carried out after enrollment. In the disciplinary areas for which recovery activities are planned (chemistry, physics, mathematics, biology), Additional Training Obligations (OFA) will be assigned exclusively to students who have given fewer than 50% correct answers to the questions. The OFA must be completed within the first year through the recovery methods established by the Course Director and the lecturers of the subjects in question.
Completing the OFA by October 31 of the year following enrollment is mandatory in order to be able to attend the corresponding official exams provided for in the Study Plan.
Methods for recovery: Based on the verification of the initial knowledge, if necessary, the Degree Course activates additional recovery courses compared to the lessons of the official courses in each of the disciplinary areas in which the students have acquired training deficiencies. To this end, the lecturers of the subjects subject to the OFA will establish 1 or 2 hours per week to be dedicated to recovery. Attendance at the recovery course is mandatory. Students who attend the recovery courses are not exempt from the obligation to attend the official courses. The training deficiency is considered to be remedied by passing a written test.
In the event of failure to complete the OFA by October 31 of the year following enrollment, students will not be able to take exams in subsequent years unless they have passed at least 18 CFU relating to the subjects provided for in the first year of the course within the basic and characterizing types.

The verification of the results obtained in the preparatory teaching activities will take place within the evaluation of the corresponding courses.

UNIVERSITY: UNIVERSITÀ DEGLI STUDI GABRIELE D'ANNUNZIO – CHIETI/PESCARA
COURSE: [L602] PHYSIOTHERAPY
SECTION: COURSE STRUCTURE INFORMATION
URL: https://unich.coursecatalogue.cineca.it/corsi/2024/611

COURSE STRUCTURE INFORMATION General information regarding the current academic year.
Duration: 3 years
Established: 2011
Status: Open
Credits: 180 CFU
Language: Italian
Type: Bachelor's Degree
Regulation: 270/04 Reform
Degree Class: L/SNT2 - Class of degrees in Healthcare professions for rehabilitation
Inter-university Course: No
Programmed Access (67 places)
Location: CHIETI (Sustainable capacity: 67)
Department: DEPARTMENT OF MEDICINE AND SCIENCE OF AGING
Website: https://www.med.unich.it/corsi-di-studio/fisioterapia-l602
Teaching Method: Conventional
Knowledge Assessment Required for Access: Yes
Course Lecturers: View list
Admission Requirements: Applicants must possess a High School Diploma or an equivalent foreign qualification. The number of students admitted to the Physiotherapy Degree Program is determined based on national and regional planning, applying the standards defined by the Faculty of Medicine and Surgery and, currently, by the School of Medicine and Health Sciences of the University of G. d'Annunzio of Chieti-Pescara.
Required knowledge for admission: knowledge of the English language at least at the A2/2 level (CEFR); basic knowledge of Biology, Chemistry, Mathematics/Physics, Logic, and General Knowledge at the High School level.
Methods for verifying the possession of such knowledge: entrance test with multiple-choice questions on elements of Biology, Chemistry, Physics/Mathematics, Logic, and General Knowledge; ongoing test of knowledge of the English language.
Criteria for assigning specific additional training obligations are currently being developed by the Curriculum Commission of the Degree Program.
Admission Procedure: The Physiotherapy Degree Program has programmed access, and admission is subject to passing a test conducted locally and programmed nationally on a date established annually by the Ministry of University and Research (MUR).
The admission procedures for the Course are indicated in the University competition announcement and include multiple-choice quizzes evaluated by an external certified body, Cineca. Given the high number of candidates participating in the entrance test compared to the number of places available, the cultural level and preparation of the winning candidates are such that the Degree Program has not yet had the need to establish procedures for recovering additional training obligations (OFA).
The deadlines for enrollment and registration are determined by the academic calendar as per the Course Regulations.

UNIVERSITY: UNIVERSITY OF STUDIES GABRIELE D'ANNUNZIO – CHIETI/PESCARA
COURSE: [L603] NURSING
SECTION: INFORMATION ON STUDY PLANS
URL: https://unich.coursecatalogue.cineca.it/corsi/2024/613

INFORMATION ON STUDY PLANS General information relating to the current academic year. Duration: 3 years. Admission year: 2011. Status: Open. Credits: 180 CFU. Language: Italian. Course Type: Bachelor's Degree. Regulatory Framework: Reform 270/04. Degree Class: L/SNT1 - Class of degrees in Health professions, nursing professions and midwifery profession. Inter-university Course: No. Programmed Access (250 places). Location: CHIETI (Sustainable student capacity: 140). Other locations: PESCARA (Sustainable student capacity: 80), VASTO (Sustainable student capacity: 30). Department: DEPARTMENT OF MEDICINE AND AGING SCIENCES. Website: https://www.med.unich.it/corsi-di-studio/infermieristica-l603. Teaching method: Conventional. Knowledge verification required for access: Yes. Course lecturers: View list. Admission requirements: Knowledge required for access. Candidates who possess a high school diploma or another equivalent qualification obtained abroad are admitted to the Nursing Degree Course. The essential and motivational pre-requisites required of a student who wishes to enroll in the Nursing Degree Course must include good interpersonal skills, flexibility, and the ability to analyze and solve problems. To be admitted to the Nursing Degree Course, candidates must possess adequate preparation in logic, general culture, and the fields of biology, chemistry, and physics. Access to the Degree Course is regulated by law 264/99 and involves a national entrance exam prepared by the MIUR, which consists of a multiple-choice test. Admission procedure: Access to the Degree Course is regulated by law 264/99 and involves a national entrance exam prepared by the MUR, which consists of a multiple-choice test. The competition announcement indicates the accredited training centers in Chieti, Pescara, and Vasto for teaching and clinical training activities. Candidates who pass the selection test must then indicate the preferred training centers, in order of preference, when enrolling. When assigning successful candidates to the chosen training centers, preference is given to the highest score obtained in the merit ranking. The Ministry of Education, University and Research (Article 6 of DM 270/2004) requires all universities to verify the initial preparation of new students, in order to allow students to assess their preparation in relation to the basic knowledge required for university studies, and to ensure that any educational deficiencies are addressed during the first year of the course. On the online website of the Degree Course, students can view any additional educational requirements, verified by the commission of the relevant lecturers based on the scores obtained in the competition ranking. The student must address the educational requirement through the following remedial activities: mandatory attendance, within the first year of the course, of 8 hours of physics, 8 hours of biology, and 8 hours of chemistry. At the end of the remedial procedures, the student will be subjected to a verification test consisting of multiple-choice questions in order to assess the required levels of competence. The OFA (Additional Educational Requirements) must be completed by the date established by the University authorities (October 31) and in any case within the first year of the course. Information on the remedial activities can be found on the online website of the Degree Course (Link to the Degree Course website). Students with educational requirements will not be able to enroll in the exams for the second-year courses until they have completed the assigned OFA. If the requirement is not met within the first year, the remedial activities will be repeated in the following academic year, once only. The syllabus for the OFA is as follows:
OFA MATHEMATICS AND PHYSICS: Real functions of real variables. Properties of powers. Exponential and logarithm. Review of trigonometry. Vector calculus. Scalar and vector quantities. Measurement errors.
OFA BIOLOGY: 1. Fundamental characteristics of the atoms and molecules that constitute the basis of the chemistry of life. In particular, be able to describe: - Carbon atoms and organic molecules. - Carbohydrates. - Lipids. - Proteins. - Nucleic acids. - Identification of biological molecules. 2. Fundamental characteristics of cellular organization. In particular, be able to describe: - The importance of cell theory. - Methods for studying cell structure. - The differences and similarities between prokaryotic and eukaryotic cells. - Structure and function of the nucleus and cell organelles with membranes. - Structure and function of the cytoskeleton. - Structure and function of the extracellular matrix.
OFA CHEMISTRY: Introduction to the study of chemistry. Units of measurement. Matter. Structure of the atom. Modern atomic theory. Atomic and molecular orbitals. Quantum numbers. Electronic configuration, Aufbau principle. In the periodic system. Ionization energy, electron affinity, and electronegativity. Chemical nomenclature. Covalent, ionic, and metallic bonds. Van der Waals and hydrogen bonds. Solids, liquids, and gases. States of aggregation of matter. Changes of state. The solid state. The liquid state. Vapor pressure of a liquid. The gaseous state. The laws of gases. Ideal gases and the equation of state. Solutions. Solubility. Concentration of solutions. Colligative properties of solutions and the van't Hoff factor. Boiling point elevation and freezing point depression. Elements of organic chemistry. The chemistry of carbon. Representation and classification of organic compounds. Hydrocarbons. Alkanes. Alkenes and alkynes. Simple functional groups (Hydroxyl, Carbonyl, Carboxyl). Complex functional groups (Ethers, Esters, Anhydrides).

Admission Requirements: Knowledge required for access. Candidates who possess a high school diploma or another degree obtained abroad, recognized as equivalent, may be admitted to the Bachelor's Degree in Techniques of Medical Radiology, Imaging, and Radiotherapy.
The pre-requisites required for students who wish to enroll in the course should include good interpersonal skills, good teamwork skills, and the ability to analyze and solve problems.
Access to the degree course is programmed according to law 264/99 and involves an entrance exam consisting of a test with multiple-choice questions.
To be admitted to the Bachelor's Degree in Techniques of Medical Radiology, Imaging, and Radiotherapy, adequate preparation in the fields of physics and mathematics is required.
Students admitted to the course with a score lower than the minimum predetermined score will be assigned additional training obligations in the disciplines of physics and mathematics, according to the methods specified in the Course Regulations.

UNIVERSITY: UNIVERSITÀ DEGLI STUDI GABRIELE D'ANNUNZIO – CHIETI/PESCARA
COURSE: [L610] LOGOPEDIA
SECTION: INFO PIANI DI STUDIO
URL: https://unich.coursecatalogue.cineca.it/corsi/2024/1784

INFO PIANI DI STUDIO General information regarding the current academic year. Duration: 3 years. Admission year: 2022. Status: Open. Credits: 180 CFU. Language: Italian. Course type: Laurea (Bachelor's Degree). Regulatory framework: Reform 270/04. Degree class: L/SNT2 - Class of degrees in Healthcare Rehabilitation Professions. Inter-university course: No. Limited access (18 places). Location: CHIETI (Sustainable student capacity: 18). Department: DEPARTMENT OF INNOVATIVE TECHNOLOGIES IN MEDICINE & ODONTOLOGY. Website: https://www.med.unich.it/corsi-di-studio/logopedia-l610
Admission requirements: Required knowledge for admission. Candidates who hold a high school diploma or another equivalent qualification obtained abroad, recognized as suitable according to current regulations (art. 6, paragraph 2, D.M. 270/04), and who possess adequate preparation and are qualified in a position eligible for the entrance exam, which, being a nationally limited-access course, will be held according to the annual provisions of the relevant Ministerial Decrees, may be admitted to the Bachelor's Degree course.
The required knowledge for admission is verified by achieving the minimum score in the entrance exam, as stipulated by current regulations. Candidates who do not reach this score will be assigned additional training obligations (OFA) to be fulfilled by attending appropriate remedial courses, as indicated by the relevant Area Teaching Council, according to the methods set out in the Course Regulations. Admission method: The Bachelor's Degree in Logopedics has a national limited number of places, and the number of places, date, and admission methods are determined annually by the University and the MIUR, through ministerial decrees, as provided for by Decree Law 264/99, in agreement with the Ministry of Health and the regional health system.
Students who wish to take the national test must hold a high school diploma.
The entrance exam is based on a multiple-choice quiz on general knowledge, mathematics, physics, biology, chemistry, and logical reasoning.
Students who need to fulfill the OFA will be exempt if they obtain 18 CFU in the basic and core subjects of the first year of the course.
Otherwise, they must attend appropriate remedial courses, as indicated by the Course Council. Currently, the only subject subject to OFA is Physics.

Admission requirements: Knowledge required for access. Students who possess a high school diploma or an equivalent foreign qualification may be admitted to the Degree Course in Techniques for Prevention in the Environment and Workplaces (TPALL). Basic knowledge of biology, chemistry, mathematics, and physics at the high school level is required. Furthermore, knowledge of the English language at least at level A2/2 (CEFR) is also required.
The student's initial preparation will be assessed through the analysis of errors found in the logic, chemistry, biology, and physics-mathematics quizzes (multiple-choice questions) administered in the entrance exam, which is common to all degree courses in the health area.
A non-selective interview may be required to highlight any gaps in the student's academic preparation in scientific subjects. These gaps may be filled by any preparatory courses that will be organized for this purpose.

INFORMATION ON STUDY PLANS General information regarding the current academic year. Duration: 3 years. Program start year: 2019. Status: Open. Credits: 180 CFU. Language: Italian. Course type: Bachelor's Degree. Regulatory framework: 270/04 reform. Degree class: L/SNT2 - Class of degrees in Rehabilitation health professions. Inter-university course: No. Limited access (23 places). Location: CHIETI (Sustainable student capacity: 23). Department: DEPARTMENT OF MEDICINE AND AGING SCIENCES. Website: https://www.med.unich.it/corsi-di-studio/ortottica-ed-assistenza-oftalmologica-l641
Admission requirements: The knowledge required for admission to the degree course in orthoptics and ophthalmic assistance should include: ability to interact with people, ability to work in a team, ability to analyze and solve problems, ability to acquire new knowledge, and critical thinking skills. Therefore, in addition to the scientific knowledge useful for the first year of the course, students should possess good motivational skills, which are essential for the training of a good Orthoptist. To be admitted to the Degree Course in Orthoptics and Ophthalmic Assistance, applicants must hold a high school diploma or another equivalent qualification obtained abroad, recognized as suitable. Furthermore, they must possess adequate initial preparation, as required by current regulations regarding access to courses with limited places at the national level.
The knowledge required for admission is verified by achieving a minimum score in the entrance exam, as required by current regulations.
Candidates who do not achieve this score will be assigned additional training obligations (OFA), which must be completed within the first year of the course by passing specific supplementary courses, according to the methods set out in the course regulations.

UNIVERSITY: UNIVERSITÀ DEGLI STUDI GABRIELE D'ANNUNZIO – CHIETI/PESCARA
COURSE: [L644] OCCUPATIONAL THERAPY
SECTION: COURSE STRUCTURE INFORMATION
URL: https://unich.coursecatalogue.cineca.it/corsi/2024/618

COURSE STRUCTURE INFORMATION General information regarding the current academic year. Duration: 3 years. Admission year: 2022. Status: Open. Credits: 180 CFU. Language: Italian. Course type: Bachelor's Degree. Regulatory framework: 270/04 reform. Degree class: L/SNT2 - Class of degrees in Health professions for rehabilitation. Inter-university course: No. Programmed access (25 places). Location: CHIETI (Sustainable capacity: 25). Department: DEPARTMENT OF NEUROSCIENCES, IMAGING AND CLINICAL SCIENCES. Website: https://www.med.unich.it/corsi-di-studio/terapia-occupazionale-l644
Admission requirements: Required knowledge for admission. Candidates who hold a high school diploma or another equivalent qualification obtained abroad, recognized as suitable according to current regulations (art. 6, paragraph 2, D.M. 270/04), and who possess adequate preparation and are qualified in a position eligible for the admission exam, may be admitted to the Bachelor's Degree course in Occupational Therapy (class L/SNT2).
Access to the course is programmed according to law 264/99. The University, in implementing the ministerial decrees issued annually for admission purposes, verifies the adequacy of general knowledge and logical reasoning skills, as well as the theoretical/practical knowledge and knowledge of current regulations specific to the discipline and functional to its subsequent professional application, as well as knowledge of scientific-mathematical culture, statistics, computer science, English, and humanities and social sciences.
Admission to the Bachelor's Degree course in Occupational Therapy (class L/SNT2) following a transfer from a different Bachelor's Degree course other than Occupational Therapy (class L/SNT2), even if belonging to the same class, or from another course of study at the same University, is possible within the limits of available places. The recognition of studies completed at degree courses of other Italian Universities, as well as the credits obtained in these courses, may be recognized after examining the curriculum transmitted by the University of origin and the programs of the courses accredited at that University.
The admission test, used to verify the possession of adequate knowledge and skills, will also be used to prepare the merit ranking and to assign additional training obligations (OFA). Specifically, students admitted to the course who obtain a score lower than 25% of the maximum score in the Biology and Physics disciplines in the entrance test will be assigned additional training obligations (OFA) to be completed during the first year of the course, with the relevant verification methods.

UNIVERSITY: UNIVERSITY OF STUDIES GABRIELE D'ANNUNZIO – CHIETI/PESCARA
COURSE: [L654] DENTAL HYGIENE
SECTION: INFO ON STUDY PLANS
URL: https://unich.coursecatalogue.cineca.it/corsi/2024/607

INFO ON STUDY PLANS General information regarding the current academic year. Duration: 3 years. Program: 2019. Status: Open. Credits: 180 CFU. Language: Italian. Course Type: Bachelor's Degree. Regulatory Reform: 270/04. Degree Class: L/SNT3 - Class of degrees in technical health professions. Inter-university Course: No. Limited Access (15 places). Location: CHIETI (Sustainable capacity: 15). Department: DEPARTMENT OF MEDICAL, ORAL AND BIOTECHNOLOGICAL SCIENCES. Website: https://www.med.unich.it/corsi-di-studio/igiene-dentale-l654
Admission Requirements: Required knowledge for access. Candidates who possess a five-year secondary school diploma or another equivalent qualification obtained abroad, recognized as suitable, and who have qualified in a favorable position in the admission ranking, in accordance with current regulations regarding access to degree courses with limited places at a national level, may be admitted to the degree course in dental hygiene. The number of students admitted to the first year of the course is determined by the competent authorities on the basis of national planning, in relation to the available facilities and teaching staff. The admission exam to the Degree Course takes place according to the methods defined by the Ministry of Education, University and Research (MIUR) and consists of multiple-choice quizzes and covers topics of Logic and General Culture, Biology, Chemistry, Mathematics and Physics.
For admission to the Degree Course, students must possess adequate initial preparation acquired in previous studies, in particular, knowledge of chemistry, biology, mathematics, physics, logic and general culture is required.
The knowledge required for access will be assessed through the entrance exam for nationally organized university courses.
In any case, the Degree Course will provide for a verification of the student's adequate preparation according to the methods defined in the Course Study Regulations. In case of ascertained deficiencies, the student will be assigned additional training obligations to be completed within the first year of the course.
Admission procedures: The number of students admitted to the Degree Course is programmed in relation to the actual availability of human resources, teaching and support staff, suitable teaching and scientific facilities and equipment (classrooms, laboratories for exercises and simulations for biomedical research), services and units operating in the local hospital structures and in the affiliated area of the National Health Service, and is annually determined by the Ministry with a specific decree. The admission procedures to the Degree Course will be indicated in the University competition announcement.
Students admitted to the Course who have obtained a score lower than 25% of the maximum score in the disciplines of Biology, Chemistry, Mathematics and Physics in the entrance test will be assigned additional training obligations (OFA). These students will be duly informed, both by email and through direct interviews, of the deficiencies found in their preparation and of the activities prepared to facilitate their recovery.
The completion of the OFA involves the assignment of individually assigned seminar-style training activities for each of the disciplines in which the student has shown deficiencies; these will be carried out during the first semester of the first year.
The verification of the results obtained in the preparatory teaching activities will take place within the evaluation of the integrated courses that include the SSDs in which the OFA have been assigned. Furthermore, pursuant to Article 49, paragraph 3, point b of the University Teaching Regulations, it is specified that students who do not fulfill the additional training obligations as reported in Article 28 of the University Teaching Regulations cannot enroll in the courses of the second year. Therefore, although enrollment in the second year is allowed, it will not be possible to take exams in the second year before completing the assigned OFA.

UNIVERSITY: UNIVERSITÀ DEGLI STUDI GABRIELE D'ANNUNZIO – CHIETI/PESCARA
COURSE: [LM38] FOREIGN LANGUAGES FOR BUSINESS AND INTERNATIONAL COOPERATION
TYPE: Master's Degree
SECTION: INFO ON STUDY PLANS
URL: https://unich.coursecatalogue.cineca.it/corsi/2024/13

INFO ON STUDY PLANS General information regarding the current academic year.
Duration: 2 years
Academic year: 2023
Status: Open
Credits: 120 CFU
Language: Italian
Course Type: Master's Degree Courses
Regulation: 270/04 Reform
Degree Class: LM-38 - Class of Master's Degrees in Modern Languages for Communication and International Cooperation
Inter-university Course: No
Open Admission: Yes
Other locations: PESCARA (Sustainable capacity: 96)
Department: DEPARTMENT OF LANGUAGES, LITERATURES AND MODERN CULTURES
Website: https://www.lingue.unich.it/didattica
Admission Requirements: Required knowledge for admission. All students holding a first-level degree (Bachelor's degree in 'Languages and Literatures' [L-11] and Bachelor's degree in 'Linguistic Mediation and Intercultural Communication' [L-12]) are admitted to the course.
Admission Procedure: ADMISSION and verification of required knowledge for admission:
Students with degrees from classes L-11 and L-12 (ex-DM 270), classes 3 and 11 (ex-DM 509), four-year degrees in Languages and Modern Literatures, and students with degrees obtained from foreign universities, whose academic record demonstrates in-depth study of two of the languages offered in the Master's degree program over a three-year period (having obtained a minimum of 26 CFU for each language), are admitted to the LM38 course in Foreign Languages for Business and International Cooperation. In any case, the board reserves the right to evaluate the suitability of the acquired skills for each individual application.
Students with degrees from other classes (of the new system ex-DM 270, of the previous system ex-DM 509, of the old four-year degrees and foreign universities) who have obtained at least 26 CFU in the first language sector, 26 CFU in the second language sector, among the following offered in the course of study, are also admitted:
L-LIN/04 LANGUAGE AND TRANSLATION - FRENCH LANGUAGE
L-LIN/07 LANGUAGE AND TRANSLATION - SPANISH LANGUAGE
L-LIN/12 LANGUAGE AND TRANSLATION - ENGLISH LANGUAGE
L-LIN/14 LANGUAGE AND TRANSLATION - GERMAN LANGUAGE
L-LIN/21 SLAVISTICS
L-OR/12 ARAB LANGUAGE AND LITERATURE
L-OR/21 LANGUAGES AND LITERATURES OF CHINA AND SOUTHEAST ASIA
At least 6 CFU in the scientific-disciplinary sectors L-LIN/01 and related fields;
At least 6 CFU in the scientific-disciplinary sectors M-STO/02 or M-GGR/01 and related fields;
At least 12 CFU for each literature related to the two chosen languages;
At least 6 CFU of INF/01 and related fields.
Candidates who do not have these requirements can obtain the missing CFUs by enrolling in individual courses at the 'D'Annunzio' University or other legally recognized universities and passing the relevant exams before submitting a request for preliminary evaluation.
Foreign students who do not have a degree obtained in Italy must certify, or demonstrate by passing a specific test, that they have Italian language skills at level B2.
In the case of a degree obtained abroad, students must bring, in addition to the certificate of value, the study plan with the course programs, translated and legalized.
The Course Council reserves the right to verify each individual case.

UNIVERSITY: UNIVERSITÀ DEGLI STUDI GABRIELE D'ANNUNZIO – CHIETI/PESCARA
COURSE: [LM41] MEDICINE AND SURGERY
TYPE: Master's Degree
CURRICULUM: Single-Cycle Program, 6 years
SECTION: INFORMATION ON STUDY PLANS
URL: https://unich.coursecatalogue.cineca.it/corsi/2024/481

INFORMATION ON STUDY PLANS General information for the current academic year. Duration: 6 years. Program start year: 2017. Status: Open. Credits: 360 CFU. Language: Italian. Course Type: Master's Degree Courses. Regulations: 270/04 Reform. Degree Class: LM-41 - Class of Master's Degrees in Medicine and Surgery. Inter-University Course: No. Programmed Access: Yes (282 places). Location: CHIETI (Sustainable capacity: 282). Department: DEPARTMENT OF MEDICINE AND AGING SCIENCES. Website: https://www.med.unich.it/corsi-di-studio/medicina-e-chirurgia-lm41. Teaching Method: Conventional. Verification of knowledge required for access: Yes. Course Lecturers: View list. Admission Requirements: Required knowledge for access. To be admitted to the Master's Degree Course in Medicine and Surgery, applicants must hold a five-year secondary school diploma or another equivalent qualification obtained abroad.
The requirements and procedures for admission to the single-cycle Master's Degree Course in Medicine and Surgery are governed by national laws and ministerial regulations.
The following are also required:
a) Possession or acquisition of adequate initial preparation, as defined annually by the Ministerial Decree regarding the subjects covered by the entrance exam for nationally programmed courses, and published in the relevant admission notice.
b) Verification of knowledge. Regarding the verification of knowledge, it is assumed that the demonstration of knowledge is fulfilled with admission to the Degree Course. The annual Ministerial Decree relating to the methods and content of the entrance exams for nationally programmed Degree Courses may determine the score of the possible minimum threshold for admission to the courses. In the event that the Ministerial Decree does not provide for and determine a minimum threshold for admission, the Degree Course, as required by Article 6 of DM 270/2004, has defined a threshold score below which Additional Training Obligations (OFA) are assigned to be fulfilled in the first year of the course. For the purposes of verifying initial knowledge, only the percentages of answers to the questions relating to the following subjects in the admission test are considered: Biology, Chemistry, Physics and Mathematics, in order to assign OFA in the most deficient area(s).
c) Additional Training Obligations (OFA) and their verification. To guarantee didactic support for students to whom specific OFA are assigned, ad hoc remedial activities are organized (integrative and additional to the activities planned for the Course). Any OFA assigned for knowledge relating to Biology, Chemistry, Physics and Mathematics will also be considered fulfilled with the successful completion, within the deadlines set by the Academic Bodies, of the relevant exam(s):
- Integrated Course of Medical Physics (for OFA in Physics and Mathematics)
- Integrated Course of Biology and Genetics (for OFA in Biology)
- Integrated Course of Chemistry and Propedeutic Biochemistry (for OFA in Chemistry)

Credits: 360 CFU.
Language: Italian.

Degree Class: LM-46 - Degree class in Dentistry and Prosthetic Dentistry.
Inter-university Course: No.
Programmed Access (46 places).

Department: DIPARTIMENTO DI TECNOLOGIE INNOVATIVE IN MEDICINA & ODONTOIATRIA.

Admission Requirements: Required knowledge for admission. Candidates who possess a five-year secondary school diploma may be admitted to the Laurea Magistrale in Dentistry and Prosthetic Dentistry (CLMOPD). Admission to the degree program is subject to passing an exam, as specified in relevant ministerial decrees outlining the methods and content of admission tests for university courses with programmed access at the national level.
The didactic organization of the CLMOPD provides that students admitted to the 1st year of the course possess adequate knowledge of the Italian language (for candidates competing for places reserved for citizens of states not belonging to the European Union) and adequate initial preparation, acquired in previous studies.
Given the above, all students who have passed the admission exam to the C.L.M., answering correctly to less than half of the maximum score provided for the individual subjects of Physics, Chemistry, and Biology, will be admitted with an educational requirement for one or more of these subjects, which they must fulfill by October 31 of the following year. Any failure to comply will be addressed as provided for in the University's didactic regulations.
In order to allow students to acquire the CFU related to the additional educational requirements, the Course Council may establish preparatory teaching activities that will be carried out during the 1st year of the course and that must be followed by students with outstanding requirements.
The verification of the results obtained in the preparatory teaching activities will take place within the evaluation of the corresponding courses. Those who do not fulfill the O.F.A. will not be able to take the exams of the 2nd year, effectively establishing a prerequisite.

Degree Class: LM-56 - Class of Master's Degrees in Economics
Inter-University Course: No

Other Locations: PESCARA (Sustainable capacity: 12)
Department: DEPARTMENT OF SOCIO-ECONOMIC, MANAGEMENT AND STATISTICAL STUDIES
Website: https://efimec.unich.it/
Admission Requirements: Required knowledge for admission. To be admitted to the LM-56 Master's Degree Course in Economics and Finance of Businesses and Ecosystems, students must possess specific curricular requirements and adequate personal preparation.
Regarding the former, students must hold a three-year Bachelor's degree, a three-year university diploma, or a foreign degree recognized as equivalent under the law.
In particular, the curricular requirements are considered automatically met:
– if the student holds a three-year Bachelor's degree in one of the following degree classes:
L-14 Legal Services Sciences
L-15 Tourism Sciences
L-16 Business and Organization Sciences
L-18 Economics and Business Management Sciences
L-33 Economics Sciences
L-36 Political and International Relations Sciences
L-37 Social Sciences for Cooperation, Development and Peace
L-41 Statistics
– or in one of the degree classes under DM 509/99 identified as equivalent to those previously indicated by the Interministerial Decree of July 9, 2009 - Equivalencies between degree classes DM 509/1999 and degree classes DM 270/2004, for the purposes of participating in public competitions - Published in the Official Gazette of June 7, 2009, No. 233;
– or, under the previous four-year system, in one of the following degrees: Degree in Economics and Commerce, Business Economics, Political Economy, Law, Political Science and equivalent degrees.
For Italian students who hold a degree in a class other than those listed above and for foreign students whose degrees cannot be explicitly traced back to the aforementioned degree classes, the curricular requirements are assessed based on an overall evaluation of the university transcript and the specific CFU obtained in the areas of economics, business, statistics-mathematics or law, as detailed in the course's didactic regulations and in section A3.b of the SUA form.
For students who meet the curricular requirements, the Course of Studies, through a specific committee, verifies the student's personal preparation, using the methods specified in the Course of Studies' Didactic Regulations.

UNIVERSITY: UNIVERSITÀ DEGLI STUDI GABRIELE D'ANNUNZIO – CHIETI/PESCARA
COURSE: [LM67] SCIENZE E TECNICHE DELLE ATTIVITÀ MOTORIE PREVENTIVE E ADATTATE
TYPE: Laurea Magistrale
SECTION: INFO PIANI DI STUDIO
URL: https://unich.coursecatalogue.cineca.it/corsi/2024/545

INFO PIANI DI STUDIO General information regarding the current academic year.
Duration: 2 years
Regulation: 2011
Status: Open
Credits: 120 CFU
Language: Italian
Course Type: Laurea Magistrale
Regulation: Riforma 270/04
Degree Class: LM-67 - Degree class in Scienze e tecniche delle attività motorie preventive e adattate
Inter-university Course: No
Open Access: Yes
Location: CHIETI (Sustainable capacity: 120)
Department: DIPARTIMENTO DI SCIENZE MEDICHE, ORALI E BIOTECNOLOGICHE
Website: https://www.med.unich.it/corsi-di-studio/scienze-e-tecniche-delle-attivita-motorie-preventive-e-adattate-area-scientifica
Teaching Method: Conventional
Verification of knowledge required for access: Yes
Course Lecturers: View list
Admission Requirements: Knowledge required for access: To be admitted to the Laurea Magistrale in Scienze e Tecniche delle Attività Motorie Preventive e Adattate, applicants must hold a Bachelor's or three-year University Diploma, or another degree obtained abroad, recognized as equivalent (as stipulated in Article 6, paragraph 2, of the DM of October 22, 2004, No. 270).
Students with a three-year Bachelor's degree in Scienze Motorie (class L-22, according to DM 270/04; or class 33, according to DM 509/99), or a four-year degree in Scienze Motorie, can enroll in the Laurea Magistrale in Scienze e Tecniche delle attività motorie preventive e adattate, with full recognition of the 180 CFU.
For students with a degree not belonging to class L-22 or L-33 (Scienze Motorie) or a Master's degree or a University Diploma, or another degree obtained abroad and recognized as equivalent according to current laws, the relevant teaching structure will carry out a specific assessment of the previous academic record and personal preparation.
The methods for verifying the student's personal preparation are defined by the didactic regulations of the course.
Admission Procedures: To be directly admitted to the Laurea Magistrale in Scienze e Tecniche delle Attività Motorie Preventive e Adattate, applicants must have obtained a three-year degree with a score of 95/110 or higher.
For those who have obtained a three-year degree with a score lower than 95/110, enrollment will be subject to passing an aptitude test, to be held at the beginning of the academic year, usually in October, which consists of multiple-choice questions on the subjects of Anatomy, Biochemistry, Physiology, Biology, and Motor Activity in different age groups.
There is no programmed number of places for the Laurea Magistrale in Scienze e Tecniche delle Attività Motorie Preventive e Adattate.

INFO PIANI DI STUDIO General information regarding the current academic year. Duration: 2 years. Admission year: 2023. Status: Open. Credits: 120 CFU. Language: Italian, English. Course type: Laurea Magistrale. Regulations: Riforma 270/04. Degree class: LM-77 - Degree class in Economics and Business Sciences. Inter-university course. Participating universities: University of Chieti and Pescara - ECONOMIA E MANAGEMENT, Cranfield University - ECONOMIA E MANAGEMENT. Admission: Open. Other locations: PESCARA (Sustainable capacity: 69). Department: DIPARTIMENTO DI ECONOMIA AZIENDALE. Website: https://clemam.unich.it/home-clemam-2226.

Admission requirements. Required knowledge for admission. Curriculum requirements: To be admitted to the course, applicants must hold a first-cycle degree or an equivalent foreign qualification. In particular, applicants with a first-cycle degree in one of the following classes will be admitted to the assessment of adequate personal preparation: Cl. 17 and Cl. 28 ex DM 509/99, L18 and L33 ex DM 270/04, or an equivalent qualification obtained at a foreign university; applicants with a second-cycle degree in one of the following classes: Cl. 64S and Cl. 84S ex DM 509/99, LM-56 and LM-77 ex DM 270/04, or an equivalent qualification obtained at a foreign university; applicants with a four-year degree from the old system (pre DM 509/99) in Economics, obtained at an Italian university or an equivalent qualification obtained at a foreign university. For those with qualifications other than the above, the course regulations define further curriculum requirements expressed in terms of CFU in specific subject areas that the student must have acquired during their previous university career. Adequate personal preparation: The course regulations define the knowledge that students must possess to be admitted to the Master's degree course in Economics and Management, as well as the methods for verifying the student's adequate personal preparation. Knowledge of the English language, in addition to Italian, is also required. To this end, the course verifies, for admission purposes, that the student has obtained at least 6 CFU in English language during their previous three-year course or possesses a certification of at least B1 level in English. The verification of these requirements takes place through a formalized process. In particular, applications for enrollment will be examined by a special commission appointed by the Department of Business Economics to verify the existence of the aforementioned requirements, in any case based on what is provided for in the Course Regulations of the Master's Degree Course.

UNIVERSITY: UNIVERSITÀ DEGLI STUDI GABRIELE D'ANNUNZIO – CHIETI/PESCARA

INFO PIANI DI STUDIO General information regarding the current academic year.
Duration: 2 years
Regulation: 2017
Status: Open
Credits: 120 CFU
Language: Italian
Course Type: Laurea Magistrale
Regulation: Reform 270/04
Degree Class: LM-78 - Degree class in Philosophical Sciences
Inter-university Course: No
Open Access: Yes
Location: CHIETI (Sustainable capacity: 20)
Department: DIPARTIMENTO DI SCIENZE FILOSOFICHE, PEDAGOGICHE ED ECONOMICO-QUANTITATIVE
Website: https://www.disfipeq.unich.it/
Admission Requirements: Required knowledge for admission. Admission to the Master's Degree Course in Philosophical Sciences at the University of Chieti-Pescara requires both specific curricular requirements and adequate personal preparation.
1. Curricular requirements.
The following individuals may apply for admission to the Master's Degree Course in Philosophical Sciences at the University of Chieti-Pescara:
1.1 Those who hold a Bachelor's Degree in Philosophy, class L-5 (ex DM 270/04), a Bachelor's Degree in Philosophy, class 29 (ex DM 509/99), or a four-year Bachelor's Degree in Philosophy ('old system') obtained from any Italian university.
1.2 Those who hold one or more Bachelor's, Specialist, or Master's degrees (ex DM 509/99 or ex DM 270/04) and who have acquired a minimum number of CFU in areas CUN 10 and 11 during their studies. The determination of the SSDs of these areas and the minimum number of CFU for each area or group of SSDs is delegated to the didactic regulations of the study course.
1.3 Those who hold one or more degrees from the 'old system' or degrees obtained abroad and who have, in substance, acquired the same requirements as in point 2.
The verification of the possession of curricular requirements will be carried out by a commission established by the Course Council of the Master's Degree Course in Philosophical Sciences.
2. Personal preparation.
Assuming the possession of the above curricular requirements, the following individuals may be admitted to the Master's Degree Course in Philosophical Sciences at the University of Chieti-Pescara:
2.1 Those who have obtained a degree, specialist degree, master's degree, or 'old system' degree with a score of 90 or higher in the philosophical, literary, psychological, sociological, or historical fields. In the case of degrees obtained abroad, the verification of the possession of a degree equivalent to the one now defined will be carried out by a commission established by the Course Council of the Master's Degree Course in Philosophical Sciences.
2.2 Those who, in the absence of the above requirement, have been positively evaluated by a special commission established by the Course Council of the Master's Degree Course in Philosophical Sciences. This commission will evaluate the knowledge and skills of the applicants in the disciplinary areas defined by the SSD M-FIL. The methods, timing, and number of assessments will be defined by the aforementioned commission.

INFO PIANI DI STUDIO General information regarding the current academic year.
Duration: 2 years

Degree Class: LM-85 - Degree class in Educational Sciences
Inter-university Course: No

Admission Requirements: Required knowledge for access. To be admitted to the Master's Degree course, applicants must hold a degree from the Old System, a Bachelor's degree, or a three-year university diploma, or another equivalent qualification obtained abroad. Furthermore, to be admitted to the course, students must (DM 270/04, art. 6, paragraphs 1 and 2) possess knowledge of the fundamental elements of pedagogical knowledge and its connections with the knowledge of other human and social sciences.
The criteria and methods for evaluating the knowledge required for access to the Master's program will be outlined in the course's didactic regulations.
Admission Procedures: ART. 4 REQUIRED KNOWLEDGE FOR ADMISSION AND ADMISSION PROCEDURES
4.  1 Required knowledge for admission. a. To be admitted to the Master's Degree course, applicants must hold a degree from the Old System, a Bachelor's degree, or a three-year university diploma, or another equivalent qualification obtained abroad.
b. Furthermore, to be admitted to the course, students must, as per DM 270/04, art. 6, paragraphs 1 and 2, possess knowledge of the fundamental elements of pedagogical knowledge and its connections with the knowledge of other human and social sciences.
c. The criteria and methods for evaluating the knowledge required for access to the Master's program are outlined in this didactic regulation and are as follows.
4.  2 Admission procedures
5.  2 a. Those who hold a Diploma in Pedagogy or Educational Sciences (prior to 509/99); or a three-year Bachelor's degree belonging to the degree class Cl-18 (ex DM 509/99) and L-19 (ex DM 270/2004) or equivalent, may enroll in the Master's Degree course in Educational Sciences (LM-85).
Assuming that the above requirements are met, those who, in the qualification that grants access to the course, have obtained a final grade of not less than 85/110 may be admitted to the Master's Degree course in Educational Sciences (LM-85).
6.  2 b. Those who hold qualifications other than those indicated above may also enroll in the Master's Degree course in Educational Sciences LM 85, but must have obtained a minimum of 30 CFU in the pedagogical-psychological-sociological-philosophical field, according to the following distribution:
a. 18 CFU in the pedagogical SSD (M-PED)
b. 6 CFU in the psychological SSD (M-PSI) or philosophical SSD (M-FIL);
c. 6 CFU in the sociological SSD (from SPS 07 to SPS 12 inclusive).
Assuming that the above requirements are met, those who, in the qualification that grants access to the course, have obtained a final grade of not less than 85/110 may be admitted to the Master's Degree course in Educational Sciences (LM-85).

Department: DIPARTIMENTO DI MEDICINA E SCIENZE DELL'INVECCHIAMENTO.
Website: https://www.med.unich.it/corsi-di-studio/scienze-infermieristiche-ed-ostetriche-area-sanitaria.
Admission requirements: Required knowledge for admission. Admission requires the possession of a degree or university diploma enabling the professions of Nurse, Pediatric Nurse, Midwife, or other equivalent qualification.
Access to the Laurea Magistrale course is programmed according to Law 264/1999 and involves an admission exam consisting of a test with multiple-choice questions.
Curriculum requirements: For professionals with a degree in the SNT/1 or L-SNT1 class, no educational debts are required.
Candidates with an enabling qualification obtained with University Diplomas may also be admitted if they have completed at least 20 CFU in the SSD MED/45 for Nurses and MED/47 for Midwives, and 50 CFU in practical training activities.
For candidates with a Diploma in Professional Nursing, Childcare Assistant, and Midwifery obtained with the previous non-university system and recognized as an equivalent qualification, the admission requirements and educational debts will be indicated in the Course Study Regulations.
Verification of personal preparation: The adequacy of personal preparation will be verified using methods indicated in the course study regulations.
Enrollment of first-level graduates is not allowed if they have outstanding educational debts, both with regard to the possession of curriculum requirements and with regard to the possession of adequate personal preparation.

Admission Requirements: Required knowledge for admission. Admission to the Master's Degree Course in Food Science and Health is conditional upon verification of the student's initial preparation, which in any case takes place after ascertaining that the curricular requirements have been met. Adequate preparation will be assessed in subjects included in the following SSDs:
BIO/13, CHIM/03, BIO/10, BIO/09, BIO/19, BIO/14, MED/42, MED/49.
The verification of personal preparation will be considered fulfilled if the degree grade is equal to or greater than 95 out of 110.
Candidates who possess one of the following qualifications may be admitted:
- Degree in Dietetics (class SNT/3 of D.M. 509/1999 or class L/SNT3 of D.M. 270/2004)
- Degree in Biomedical Laboratory Techniques (class SNT/3 of D.M. 509/1999 or class L/SNT3 of D.M. 270/2004)
- Degree in Food Science and Technology (class 20 of D.M. 509/1999 or L-26 D.M. 270/2004)
- Degree in Biotechnology (class 1 of D.M. 509/1999 or L-2 of D.M. 270/2004)
- Degree in Biological Sciences (class 12 of D.M. 509/1999 or L-13 of D.M. 270/2004)
- Master's Degrees in Biology (class 6/S of D.M. 509/1999 or class LM-6 of D.M. 270/2004)
- Master's Degrees in Biotechnology (classes 7/S, 8/S, 9/S of D.M. 509/1999 or classes LM-7, LM-8, LM-9 of D.M. 270/2004)
- Master's Degrees in Pharmacy and Pharmaceutical Industry (class 14/S of D.M. 509/1999 or class LM-13 D.M. 270/2004)
- Master's Degree in Food Science and Technology (class 78/S of D.M. 509/1999 or LM-70 of D.M. 270/2004)
- Master's Degree in Agricultural Science and Technology (class 77/S D.M. 509/1999 or LM-69 of D.M. 270/2004)
Enrollment for students coming from Degree Courses (or other equivalent qualifications obtained abroad), other than those indicated, will be subject to having at least 60 CFU:
- 20 CFU among: FIS/07, SECS-S/01, SECS-S/02; CHIM/01, CHIM/03, CHIM/06; AGR/13; INF/01, CHIM/09, CHIM/10, AGR/15
- 30 CFU among BIO/09, BIO/13, BIO/10, BIO/14, BIO/16, BIO/19;
- 10 CFU among MED/42, MED/49, IUS/01, MED/44, MED/07
The minimum required knowledge in the various disciplines, as well as the specific details of the test, will be detailed in the Course Study Regulations, as 'cultural requirements for admission' and published in the Study Guide.
The number of students admitted to the Master's Degree Course in Food Science and Health is programmed based on the availability of teaching staff, teaching facilities (classrooms, laboratories) that can be used for conducting practical activities and, above all, the availability of internship places (mandatory), in accordance with the recommendations of the European Union, applying the parameters and directives established by the University and the coordination structures.

UNIVERSITY: UNIVERSITÀ DEGLI STUDI GABRIELE D'ANNUNZIO – CHIETI/PESCARA
COURSE: [M680] HEALTH PROFESSIONS OF REHABILITATION SCIENCES
TYPE: Laurea Magistrale
SECTION: INFO PIANI DI STUDIO
URL: https://unich.coursecatalogue.cineca.it/corsi/2024/4109

INFO PIANI DI STUDIO General information regarding the current academic year.
Duration: 2 years
Academic year: 2024
Status: Open
Credits: 120 CFU
Language: English
Course type: Laurea Magistrale
Regulation: Riforma 270/04
Degree class: LM/SNT2 - Degree class in Rehabilitation Sciences of Health Professions
Inter-university course: No
Programmed access (20 places)
Location: CHIETI (Sustainable capacity: 20)
Department: DIPARTIMENTO DI MEDICINA E SCIENZE DELL'INVECCHIAMENTO
Admission requirements: Required knowledge for access. Candidates who hold a degree enabling them to practice health professions included in the SNT2 class (podiatrist, physiotherapist, speech therapist, orthoptist - ophthalmology assistant, therapist of neuro and psychomotor skills of the developmental age, technician of education and psychiatric and psychosocial rehabilitation, occupational therapist, professional educator) or the corresponding professional qualification or equivalent university degree, or another degree obtained abroad, recognized as suitable according to current regulations, may be admitted to the Laurea Magistrale in Health Professions of Rehabilitation Sciences.
Since this is a Laurea Magistrale course in English, another requirement for admission is knowledge of the English language at the B2 level.
The B2 level is the fourth level of English (upper intermediate) established by the Common European Framework of Reference for Languages (CEFR), a system for evaluating language proficiency at various levels determined by the European Council.
Knowledge of the English language (B2 level) will be assessed through the entrance test and will be further improved during the course through the use of scientific English (reading and analysis of texts and scientific articles, use of technical jargon, etc.).
For foreign students, adequate knowledge of the Italian language (at least B2 level of the CEFR) is also required for admission.
Access to the Laurea Magistrale course is limited and regulated by law 264/99 and involves an entrance exam in English, prepared according to ministerial guidelines, aimed at verifying the personal preparation and skills acquired by the student in the fundamental areas of rehabilitation (physiotherapy, speech therapy, orthoptics and ophthalmology assistance, occupational therapy, etc.).
The date, the methods of carrying out the entrance exam, and the number of places available are defined annually by the Ministry of University and Research (MUR) and published each year in a specific admission notice issued by the University.
The pre-requisites required for students who wish to enroll in the course include, in addition to knowledge of the English language (CEFR B2), a good aptitude for human contact, a good aptitude for teamwork, and the ability to analyze and solve problems.
All the knowledge required to access the course (both basic and specific skills) will be clearly described and publicized on the website of the DMSI, which is the main department.

UNIVERSITY: UNIVERSITÀ DEGLI STUDI GABRIELE D'ANNUNZIO – CHIETI/PESCARA
COURSE: [OBC7] BENI CULTURALI
SECTION: INFO PIANI DI STUDIO
URL: https://unich.coursecatalogue.cineca.it/corsi/2024/63

INFO PIANI DI STUDIO General information regarding the current academic year. Duration: 3 years. Degree program established in: 2014. Status: Open. Credits: 180 CFU. Language: Italian. Type: Degree Course. Regulatory Framework: Reform 270/04. Degree Class: L-1 - Class of Cultural Heritage Degrees. Inter-university Course: No. Open Admission: Yes. Location: CHIETI (Sustainable capacity: 66). Department: DIPARTIMENTO DI LETTERE, ARTI E SCIENZE SOCIALI. Admission Requirements: Required knowledge for admission. Students with Italian or foreign high school diplomas (with verification of the equivalence of the degree) and knowledge of at least one language of the European Union, in addition to Italian, can be enrolled in the historical-artistic program. Students who demonstrate gaps in their knowledge will have to make up for the corresponding educational deficiencies by attending specific zero-credit courses organized within the Degree Program.
Students with Italian or foreign high school diplomas (with verification of the equivalence of the degree) who possess a good knowledge of the history and culture of the ancient world and knowledge of at least one language of the European Union, in addition to Italian, can be enrolled in the archaeological program. It is also required that they be willing to participate in practical exercises and educational activities in the field.
Students who do not meet the requirements indicated above will agree on the admission procedures with the course coordinators.
Students who demonstrate gaps in their knowledge, particularly regarding the knowledge of Greek or Latin, will have to make up for the corresponding educational deficiencies by attending specific zero-credit courses organized within the Degree Program.

UNIVERSITY: UNIVERSITÀ DEGLI STUDI GABRIELE D'ANNUNZIO – CHIETI/PESCARA
COURSE: [STAR] HISTORICAL STUDIES AND CULTURAL HERITAGE FROM ANTIQUITY TO THE CONTEMPORARY ERA
TYPE: Master's Degree
SECTION: INFO COURSE STRUCTURES
URL: https://unich.coursecatalogue.cineca.it/corsi/2024/5118

INFO COURSE STRUCTURES General information for the current academic year Duration: 2 years. Year: 2024. Status: Open. Credits: 120 CFU. Language: Italian. Course Type: Master's Degree. Regulations: Reform 270/04. Degree Class: LM-5 R - Archiving and Library Science. Interclass Degree: LM-84 R - Historical Sciences. Inter-University Course: No. Open Access: Yes. Location: CHIETI (Sustainable capacity: 50). Department: DEPARTMENT OF LITERATURE, ARTS AND SOCIAL SCIENCES. Teaching Method: Blended/mixed mode. Knowledge Verification Required for Access: No. Course Lecturers: View list. Admission Requirements: Required knowledge for access. To be admitted to the Master's Degree program, applicants must hold a Bachelor's degree in Cultural Heritage (class L01), Visual Arts, Music, Performing Arts and Fashion (class L03), Philosophy (class L05), Geography (class L06), Literature (class L10), Modern Languages and Cultures (class L11), Linguistic Mediation (class L12), Tourism Sciences (class L15), Education and Training Sciences (class L19), Communication Sciences (class L20), Political and International Relations (class L36), Sociology (class L40), History (class L42). It will also be possible to access the Master's Degree program from other degree courses, even under the old system, provided that at least 15 CFU in total have been obtained in the scientific-disciplinary sectors M-STO related to area 11, or at least two exams in ancient, medieval, modern or contemporary history and their equivalents under the old system.
